### [1] [Folding an e-graph in pure egglog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01249)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文报告了在纯egglog中定义和实现catamorphism（一种通用的折叠操作）的初步尝试，讨论了通过阶段性推理规则避免值重新传播的实现技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在e-graphs上下文中实现折叠操作可以带来免费的记忆化和计算减少（因为子问题定义在e-classes而非具体子项上），但目前缺乏通用的实现方案。

Method: 在纯egglog中定义和实现catamorphism，通过阶段性推理规则来避免更新值的重新传播。

Result: 提出了一种可重用的实现技巧，能够有效避免推理规则中的值重新传播问题。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在egglog中实现通用折叠操作的可行性，为e-graph上的递归计算提供了初步的方案。

Abstract: Folding over a recursive data structure (also known as "catamorphism") is an essential operation in functional programming. It allows one to declaratively define a recursive computation by focusing on how solutions of subproblems are combined into the solution of the parent problem. Folding in the context of e-graphs can have several benefits. Not only do we get memoization for free, but also computation is drastically reduced since the subproblems are defined over e-classes as opposed to concrete sub-terms. This short report presents our initial attempt at defining and implementing catamorphism, a general notion of a fold, in pure egglog. We discuss a reusable implementation trick to stage the inference rules so that there is no re-propagation of updated values.

</details>


### [2] [Trustworthy Runtime Verification via Bisimulation (Extended Experience Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01363)
*Ryan G. Scott,Ivan Perez,Alwyn E. Goodloe,Mike Dodds,Robert Dockins*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CopilotVerifier，它通过符号执行和SMT求解技术，自动验证Copilot运行时监控编译器生成的C代码与原始DSL规范之间的行为等价性和无崩溃性，以中等代价显著提升了编译器保障水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键系统中使用运行时监控时，监控代码的正确性至关重要。然而，人工审计员难以确信编译后的监控程序不会崩溃且实现了Copilot语义所要求的行为，需要为每段部署代码提供令人信服的保障论证。

Method: CopilotVerifier与Copilot编译器并行运行，通过建立双模拟（bisimulation）并分解为一组验证条件来证明正确性。利用两个SMT支撑技术：面向LLVM的Crucible符号执行库和What4求解器接口库。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过构建在现有工具之上，可以以中等成本实现显著增强的编译器保障水平。

Conclusion: 该工作为最终目标——生成令人工审计员信服的形式化保障论证——铺平了道路，证明了在现有工具基础上构建自动化验证以提升安全关键系统可信度的可行性。

Abstract: When runtime verification is used to monitor safety-critical systems, it is essential that monitoring code behaves correctly. The Copilot runtime verification framework pursues this goal by automatically generating C monitor programs from a high-level DSL embedded in Haskell. In safety-critical domains, every piece of deployed code must be accompanied by an assurance argument that is convincing to human auditors. However, it is difficult for auditors to determine with confidence that a compiled monitor cannot crash and implements the behavior required by the Copilot semantics.
  In this paper we describe CopilotVerifier, which runs alongside the Copilot compiler, generating a proof of correctness for the compiled output. The proof establishes that a given Copilot monitor and its compiled form produce equivalent outputs on equivalent inputs, and that they either crash in identical circumstances or cannot crash. The proof takes the form of a bisimulation broken down into a set of verification conditions. We leverage two pieces of SMT-backed technology: the Crucible symbolic execution library for LLVM and the What4 solver interface library. Our results demonstrate that dramatically increased compiler assurance can be achieved at moderate cost by building on existing tools. This paves the way to our ultimate goal of generating formal assurance arguments that are convincing to human auditors.

</details>

### [3] [Data Comics for Education: Evaluating Effectiveness, Benefits, and the Ethics of AI-Assisted Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02361)
*Zirui Shan,Vanessa Echeverria,Yuheng Li,Yi-Shan Tsai,Roberto Martinez-Maldonado*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过60名大学生的被试内实验，对比了传统可视化图表与利用生成式AI辅助创建的数据漫画在教育场景中的效果。结果显示数据漫画在信息检索和理解任务中表现更优，尤其在洞察理解方面，且不受学生先前可视化素养水平的影响。学生认为数据漫画更具吸引力和易理解性，但对其中的AI生成错误信息和所有权问题表示担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在数据可视化解读方面存在素养不足的问题。数据漫画作为一种有前景的可视化媒介，其教育价值缺乏充分的实证检验，而传统的数据漫画制作成本较高。生成式AI的进步为规模化创建数据漫画提供了可行方案，因此需要系统评估其在教育环境中的有效性。

Method: 采用被试内设计，招募60名大学生，对比传统可视化图表与利用生成式AI工具辅助创建的数据漫画，在信息检索和理解两类任务上的表现。同时收集学生的定性反馈。

Result: 数据漫画在所有任务上均优于传统可视化，尤其在洞察理解任务中优势显著，且这种优势独立于学生先前的可视化素养水平。学生主观评价数据漫画更具吸引力和更易理解，但也表达了对AI生成错误信息和所有权问题的担忧。

Conclusion: 数据漫画有潜力成为教育中有效的数据传播工具，但在AI辅助创作过程中需要关注伦理问题（错误信息和所有权）。

Abstract: In today's data-driven world, students often struggle with interpreting visualisations due to limited visualisation literacy. Data comics have emerged as a promising medium to enhance engagement and understanding, but their educational value has seen little empirical examination, partly due to the effort required to create them. Recent advances in Generative AI (GenAI) offer a scalable solution to this challenge. We conducted a within-subjects study with 60 university students, comparing conventional visualisations with data comics, created with assistance from GenAI tools, across information retrieval and comprehension tasks. Students consistently performed better with data comics, particularly in insight comprehension tasks, independent of prior visualisation literacy. Students also commented data comics as more engaging and easier to understand, though concerns were raised about GenAI-driven misinformation and ownership. Our findings highlight the potential of data comics as a potentially effective tool for data communication in education, while underscoring the need to address ethical concerns related to AI-assisted creation.

</details>


### [4] [ExPerT: Personalizing LLM Responses to Users' Domain Expertise via Query-Wise Semantic and Keystroke Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01242)
*Yeji Park,Jiwon Tark,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ExPerT是一个查询级个性化框架，通过结合语义线索（查询文本）和行为线索（击键动态）推断用户的查询领域专业知识水平，并据此自适应调整LLM响应的详细程度、术语和概念复杂度。用户研究显示，与最强基线相比，专业知识推断误差降低65.7%，响应满意度提升17.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖静态用户档案或纯文本信号，无法捕捉用户在不同查询上的专业知识差异，导致响应内容与实际用户水平不匹配。

Method: 提出ExPerT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义-行为专业知识推断模块，通过上下文提示联合解析查询文本和击键动态；(2) 专业知识条件响应生成，根据推断的专业水平调整响应的详细程度、术语和概念复杂度。

Result: 40名参与者、1270条查询的用户实验中，ExPerT的专业知识推断误差相比最强基线降低65.7%（MAE=0.398 vs 1.162），5点Likert量表上响应满意度提升17.52%（从3.71到4.36）。

Conclusion: 结合语义和行为线索能够更准确地推断用户查询级专业知识水平，进而生成个性化响应，显著优于传统静态档案或纯文本方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by end users, yet existing personalization methods relying on static profiles or text-only signals fail to capture query-specific expertise variation. We present ExPerT, a query-wise personalization framework that adapts LLM responses to users' query domain expertise by combining semantic and behavioral cues. ExPerT consists of two key components: (i) a semantic-behavioral expertise inference module that jointly interprets query text and keystroke dynamics via in-context LLM prompting, and (ii) an expertise-conditioned response generation that adapts the level of detail, terminology, and conceptual complexity. Our user study with 40 participants and 1270 queries demonstrated that ExPerT reduced expertise inference error by 65.7% compared to the strongest baseline (MAE = 0.398 vs. 1.162) and improved response satisfaction by 17.52% (from 3.71 to 4.36) on a 5-point Likert scale.

</details>


### [5] [Mitigating Confirmation Bias through Hand-Drawing Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01359)
*Chenyu Lin,Cindy Xiong,Icy Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了通过展示可视化图表的动态手绘过程（相比静态图表）能否减少确认偏误、提高数据解读准确性。实验表明，手绘视频帮助参与者更准确地解读数据，即使数据与先验信念冲突，并能减少信念一致性错误、增加信念超越性反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化解读常受先验信念影响而产生确认偏误，现有方法缺乏有效缓解手段。作者希望探索一种具身化的教学方法——让观众观察图表被手绘构建的动态过程——来减轻这种偏误。

Method: 进行了一项对比实验，比较静态柱状图与手绘构建视频，在数据与参与者先验信念一致或冲突的两种情境下，测量解读准确性、信念一致性错误和信念超越性反应。

Result: 手绘视频帮助参与者准确解读数据，即使在数据与先验信念冲突时依然有效；减少了信念一致性错误，并增加了信念超越性反应。

Conclusion: 暴露可视化的构建过程能够支持更准确的推理，缓解确认偏误的影响，为设计偏误缓解数据界面提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Understanding data visualizations is essential for informed decision-making, yet interpretation is often shaped and even distorted by prior beliefs. We investigate whether an embodied pedagogical approach, in which viewers observe the dynamic hand-drawing of a visualization, can mitigate confirmation bias and improve interpretation accuracy. We conducted a study comparing static bar charts to videos in which charts are constructed through hand-drawing, across contexts that either align with or challenge participants' prior beliefs. The results indicate that hand-drawn videos helped participants accurately interpret data, even when the data conflicted with their prior beliefs. This approach also reduced belief-consistent errors and increased belief-overriding responses. These findings suggest that exposing the construction process of a visualization supports more accurate reasoning and mitigates the influence of confirmation bias. Consequently, this work introduces a promising design space for bias-mitigating data interfaces.

</details>


### [6] [Mind the Trust Gap: Identifying (Mis)alignments in Teacher-Student Views Toward Control and Agency in K-12 Classroom AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01506)
*Tomohiro Nagashima,Lisa Siegrist,Niklas Scholz,Shintaro Sato,Martina Vincoli,Man Su*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过速配实验和配对分析，发现德国K-12课堂中师生在AI决策控制权、AI信任度及社会情感学习方面存在显著观点不一致，揭示了师生关系对AI交互偏好的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未能充分揭示K-12课堂中教师与学生关于AI的观点是否一致，尤其缺少对师生之间观点对齐/错位的深入理解。

Method: 采用故事板速配研究方法，对德国16名学生和15名教师进行实验，并通过显式配对匹配分析师生观点。

Result: 师生在AI信任度、社会情感学习等关键主题上存在观点错位；师生关系在AI使用之外也对观点和互动产生重要影响。

Conclusion: 师生-AI互动的偏好具有复杂性，取决于利益相关者之间的动态关系；需要弥合视角差距的策略。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based technologies have been integrated into school classrooms where multiple stakeholders (with different roles) interact with each other, it is critical to deeply understand stakeholder views in the classroom. In particular, prior work has not fully uncovered how teachers' and school students' views might or might not align well with each other, especially in K-12 classrooms. We conducted a speed-dating study using storyboards with 16 school students and 15 school teachers in Germany to investigate alignments and misalignments between their views on student-AI decision-making control in K-12 classroom. Through an explicit pair-matching analysis, we found that students and teachers had misaligned views on several key topics, including how much they trust AI and social and emotional aspects of student learning with AI. Findings also revealed the importance of teacher-student relationships outside of AI use that shape stakeholders' views and interactions. We discuss potential reasons for the observed misaligned views and strategies to fill the perspective gaps. This study illustrates the complexities of preferences in teacher-student-AI interactions that depend on the dynamic relations among the stakeholders.

</details>


### [7] [OrchestrXR: A Multi-Agent System for Idea-to-Prototype XR Study Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01588)
*Shuqi Liao,Chenfei Zhu,Karthik Ramani,Voicu Popescu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OrchestrXR是一个多智能体人机协作工作流，用于将XR研究的初始想法快速转化为可运行的Unity原型，支持研究设计、场景生成和交互生成的阶段性可控创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR研究从初始想法到可运行原型的转化过程在实验设计、场景构建和交互实现之间高度碎片化，缺乏统一的创作支持工具。

Method: 提出OrchestrXR，采用多智能体人类-AI协作工作流，通过结构化模式、多智能体编排和交互式人机界面，将研究想法分阶段（研究设计、场景生成、交互生成）转化为基于Unity的原型。

Result: 12名XR研究者的用户研究表明，OrchestrXR能够有效支持早期阶段的XR研究创作，并在各阶段之间保持强意图一致性。

Conclusion: OrchestrXR验证了多智能体人机协作工作流在XR研究原型创作中的有效性，为解决XR研究开发碎片化问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) has become an important interaction paradigm in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI). XR studies are used to investigate interaction, perception, and user behavior in immersive environments, and typically involve experimental tasks, 3D scenes, and interactive logic. However, turning an initial XR study idea into a runnable prototype remains fragmented across study design, scene construction, and interaction implementation. We present OrchestrXR, a multi-agent human-AI workflow for early-stage idea-to-prototype XR study authoring. Rather than treating XR study creation as one-shot generation, OrchestrXR supports a controllable workflow across study design, scene generation, and interaction generation through structured schemas, multi-agent orchestration, and interactive human-agent interfaces, producing a Unity-based prototype from a researcher's idea. A user study with 12 XR researchers suggests that OrchestrXR provides effective support for early-stage XR study authoring with strong intent preservation across stages.

</details>


### [8] [Made to Feel: How Designers Bring Emotions into Affective Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01593)
*Yixin Bai,Ziyi Wang,Keke Wu,Fumeng Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对15位可视化从业者的半结构化访谈，揭示了情感在设计过程中的作用机制：情感为观众提供入口、参与和结果三种功能；设计师从数据、设计和受众三个维度处理情感；情感意图通常在设计过程中涌现而非预先规划，情感影响源于设计选择的累积而非单一视觉元素；同时讨论了伦理考量和评估挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感可视化研究日益增多，但设计师如何在可视化工作中融入情感这一过程尚未被探索。

Method: 对15位可视化从业者进行半结构化访谈，采用混合主题分析法进行分析。

Result: 识别出情感的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）；设计师处理情感的三个维度（数据、设计、受众）及相关设计策略；设计过程中的伦理考量；发现情感意图多在过程中涌现，情感影响源于设计选择的累积。

Conclusion: 情感在可视化设计中具有多重功能，其引入过程是涌现性和累积性的，评估仍是主要挑战，需要关注设计伦理。

Abstract: Affective visualization is increasingly studied in visualization research, yet how designers bring emotions into their visualization work remains unexplored. This paper addresses this gap through semi-structured interviews with 15 visualization practitioners. Using hybrid thematic analysis, we identify: (1) three functions that emotions can serve for viewers (entry, engagement, outcome); (2) three facets of how designers work with emotion (data, design, audience), along with design strategies; and (3) ethical considerations in the design process. We also observe that affective intent often emerges during the design process rather than being planned from the outset, and that emotional impact arises from accumulated design choices rather than isolated visual elements. Finally, we highlight evaluation as a key challenge identified by our participants.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating Glanceable Multi-Device Family Health Tracking with Smartwatches and Home Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01618)
*Lucas M. Silva,Evropi Stefanidi,Aehong Min,Franceli L. Cibrian,Jesus A. Beltran,Cassie Zeiler,Sabrina E. B. Schuck,Kimberley D. Lakes,Gillian R. Hayes,Daniel A. Epstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过9周实地实验，对比智能手表、家庭显示器及二者组合三种设计对家庭情绪与目标追踪的影响。结果表明，组合设计显著提升追踪频率，智能手表支持随时追踪与远程感知，家庭显示器促进家庭协作与包容非参与者。多设备冗余满足家庭成员的多样化偏好、移动规律与日常习惯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适计算领域已有大量个人健康追踪研究，但对多设备家庭信息学（family informatics）——健康管理本质上是协作性的——了解不足。作者旨在探索家庭如何采用和感知跨情境的共享健康数据普适访问。

Method: 12个家庭共44人参与了为期9周的实地实验，交替使用三种设计：仅智能手表、仅家庭显示器、二者组合。通过日志分析（追踪频率、时间模式、地点）和访谈分析（用户体验与协作行为）进行评估。

Result: 带家庭显示器的设计相比仅智能手表，显著提高了情绪和目标的追踪频率；追踪在下午达到高峰、周末下降、家中发生频率是其他场所的2.6倍；儿童追踪比成人更一致。智能手表支持机会性追踪和远程感知，家庭显示器则提醒家庭成员自我追踪并在家庭日常中促进协作，包容不使用可穿戴设备的成员。

Conclusion: 多设备冗余能适应同一家庭成员在规律、移动性和设备偏好上的多样性。作者提出通过包容性、可瞥视且可自适应普适数据共享的多设备家庭信息学设计机会。

Abstract: While ubiquitous computing research has explored diverse devices for personal health tracking, we know less about multi-device designs for family informatics, where health management is inherently collaborative. To understand how families adopt and perceive ubiquitous access to shared health data across contexts, we evaluated smartwatch-only, home display-only, and combined designs for tracking moods and goals, domains central to family health behavior regulation. 44 people across 12 families alternated between these designs over nine weeks. Log analysis revealed that mood tracking and goal reporting were significantly more frequent with the home display present compared to smartwatch-only use, despite an overall decline in mood tracking over time. Tracking peaked in afternoons, dropped on weekends, and occurred 2.6X more at home, with children tracking more consistently than adults across all designs. From interview analysis, we learned how family data glanceability on smartwatches supported opportunistic tracking and awareness while apart, whereas displays reminded families to self-track and collaborate during home routines including members that avoided wearables (e.g., non-participants). Multi-device redundancy accommodated diversity in routines, mobility patterns, and device preferences among members in the same family. We discuss opportunities for multi-device family informatics that accommodates different preferences through inclusive, glanceable, and adaptable ubiquitous data sharing.

</details>


### [10] [From Answer Generators to Reasoning Facilitators: Designing AI Tutors for Mathematical Reasoning in High-Stakes Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01692)
*Yuming Feng,Yuan Tian,Erica Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究介绍并评估了AITutor系统，该系统将理论教学机制转化为具体UI功能，帮助初中生备战中考。通过混合方法三角验证（7379条遥测事件、8次情境观察、10次访谈），发现学生在时间压力下主动抵制传统苏格拉底式对话，将"答案优先"捷径用作诊断检查点。研究展示了分层工作示例、步骤链接可视化、元认知支架等功能如何降低推理修复的交互成本，并贡献了"以推理为中心的产品循环"设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）快速融入教育技术，可能将数学学习简化为单纯的答案生成，忽视推理过程的培养。本研究旨在探索如何设计交互系统，在备考高压场景下仍能支持学生的数学推理能力发展。

Method: 采用混合方法三角验证：收集7,379条遥测事件、进行8次情境观察和10次访谈。在12名初中生中部署AITutor系统进行实地研究，系统包含分层工作示例、步骤链接可视化和元认知支架等特征。

Result: 学生在时间压力下主动抵制传统苏格拉底式对话，将"答案优先"策略重新用作诊断检查点。分层工作示例、步骤链接可视化、元认知支架等功能有效降低了推理修复的交互成本。

Conclusion: 提出了"以推理为中心的产品循环"框架，为设计AI系统提供 actionable 启示，使其在真实场景中结构性地支持数学推理的检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into educational technology threatens to reduce mathematical learning to mere answer generation. This paper presents a generative study, usability study, and 12-participant field deployment of AITutor, an interactive system that translates theoretical pedagogical mechanisms into concrete user interface features. We explore how junior-high students preparing for high-stakes exams (Zhongkao) interact with AI tutoring. Through mixed-methods triangulation (7,379 telemetry events, 8 contextual observations, 10 interviews), we reveal that students actively resist traditional Socratic dialogue under time pressure, repurposing "answer-first" shortcuts as vital diagnostic checkpoints. We demonstrate how features like layered worked examples, step-linked visual grounding, and metacognitive scaffolding lower the interaction cost of reasoning repair. We contribute a "Reasoning-Centered Product Loop," offering actionable implications for designing AI that structurally supports the inspection, local repair, curriculum verification, and delayed retrieval of mathematical reasoning in the wild.

</details>


### [11] [Adapting CCDF Plots for Visualizing Ordinal Regression Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01747)
*Abhraneel Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用修改后的互补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化序数回归模型的结果，以解决序数回归模型因结果难以直观展示而采用率低的问题，并证明mCCDF能像处理度量数据的方法一样直观地传达关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序数回归模型在假设上比线性模型或ANOVA更宽松，更适合分析李克特量表等序数数据，但因其结果难以直观可视化和传达关键信息，导致采用率有限。作者旨在解决这一可视化瓶颈，推动序数回归模型的更广泛应用。

Method: 提出使用修改后的互补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化序数回归模型的结果，并通过实例展示如何利用mCCDF像传统将序数数据视为度量数据的分析方法一样，清晰传达相同的核心研究发现。

Result: mCCDF图能够有效可视化序数回归模型的结果，使研究者能够以直观的方式传达与将序数数据视为度量数据（如线性模型/ANOVA）相同的分析结论。

Conclusion: mCCDF图为序数回归模型的结果可视化提供了一种有效的解决方案，有望降低该方法的理解门槛，促进其在可视化及相关领域的更广泛采用。

Abstract: Cumulative-link ordinal regression models are an alternative approach for analysing ordinal data such as Likert items, which are widely used in Visualization (and other related fields like HCI, psychology etc.). There are many researchers who are strong proponents of this approach, as it makes less stringent assumptions about the data, compared to the more commonly used linear model or ANOVA. Yet, ordinal regression models have seen limited adoption. I posit that one possible reason for this might be due to the difficulty in visually representing the results from such models, and in communicating the key takeaways in an intuitive manner. I propose the use of (modified) Complementary Cumulative Distribution Function (mCCDF) plots to visualize the results of ordinal regression models, and demonstrate how the same takeaways that researchers present from analyses which treat ordinal data as metric can be easily communicated using mCCDFs.

</details>


### [12] [A Social Norms Approach to Youth Social Media Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01807)
*JaeWon Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文认为，年轻人在社交媒体上难以实现真实自我表达和建立信任，根本原因不是个人选择问题，而是平台规范、同伴感知和集体信念等规范性障碍。作者提出社会规范研究方法，主张平台规范导致多元无知、设计干预受现有规范制约、以及"社交媒体"的社会定义限制了替代性平台的发展，最终通过让年轻人直接参与设计和构建一个以建立信任连接为目标的独立平台来验证这些主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人渴望在社交媒体上获得真实自我表达、减少评判和增强人际信任，但实际上很少能做到这一点。现有研究多从个体层面解释这一现象，但作者认为真正的障碍是规范性的而非个体性的，即年轻人的社交行为更多受平台规范、同伴感知和集体信念约束，而非个人选择。

Method: 论文采用社会规范研究方法，围绕三个核心主张展开：(1)通过多元无知理论分析平台规范对个体行为的约束；(2)论证设计干预本身受现有规范塑造，隐私等关系性目标需作为社会规范而非个人设置来处理；(3)批判"社交媒体"等同于少数主流平台的社会定义。最终方法是通过让年轻人直接参与设计和构建一个基于证据的独立平台，其功能一致性地传达"建立信任连接"的空间定位。

Result: 论文提出了三个核心发现：第一，平台规范导致多元无知，年轻人会执行自己私下反对的规范；第二，设计特征的效果取决于周围规范环境，而非仅靠功能本身；第三，"社交媒体"被狭隘地等同于少数主流平台，限制了政策和设计思维，使其局限于"减轻危害"而非"主动构想替代方案"。

Conclusion: 要解决年轻人在社交媒体上难以真实表达和建立信任的问题，需要从规范性层面而非个体层面进行干预。仅仅在现有主流平台上修补功能是不够的，需要重新定义社交媒体的社会角色，让年轻人直接参与设计一个以信任连接为核心价值的新型平台。

Abstract: Young people consistently say they want authentic self-expression, less judgment, and more interpersonal trust on social media, yet they rarely manage to engage that way. My dissertation argues that the obstacle is normative rather than individual: how youth engage is governed less by personal choice than by platform norms, peer perception, and beliefs about how others behave. I take a social norms approach to youth social media design organized around three claims. First, platform norms constrain individual behavior, producing a pluralistic ignorance in which youth enact norms they privately reject. Second, design interventions are themselves shaped by existing norms, so whether a feature works depends on the environment around it, which means relational goals such as privacy must be treated as social norms rather than individual settings. Third, a societal norm about what ``social media'' is -- equating it with a few mainstream platforms -- confines policy and design to mitigating those platforms rather than actively envisioning supportive alternatives. Together these claims motivate my dissertation research: engaging youth directly in designing and building an evidence-based independent platform whose features consistently signal that building trusted connections is what the space is for.

</details>


### [13] [Visual Analytics of Neighborhood Attribute Profiles for Exploring Structural Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02163)
*Kohei Arimoto,Masahiko Itoh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于降维的可视分析方法，揭示节点邻域属性特征形成的高维特征空间的真实拓扑结构。通过分析企业间交易网络发现，结构角色可能形成具有密度偏差的复杂非线性流形；行业分类与特征空间的对比表明：供应链层级连续过渡、语义相同的类别在交易网络中可被清晰区分、同一行业标签可能分裂为多个区域。这些发现挑战了"相同语义意味着相似结构角色"的假设，并指出需要与流形拓扑对齐的新型相似度度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法隐式假设特征空间均匀且连续，可能无法准确反映真实网络拓扑结构。本文旨在通过可视分析方法揭示高维特征空间的真实拓扑结构，并检验行业分类与结构角色之间的对应关系。

Method: 采用降维技术对节点邻域属性特征形成的高维特征空间进行可视化分析，以企业间交易网络为案例数据，将特征空间与行业分类标签进行对比分析。

Result: (1) 供应链层级呈现连续过渡而非离散分布；(2) 通用语义下相同的类别在实际交易网络中可被清晰分离；(3) 单一行业标签可能在特征空间中分裂为多个区域。

Conclusion: 语义相同不一定意味着结构角色相似，现有相似度度量存在局限，需要开发与流形拓扑对齐的新型度量方法。

Abstract: Exploring similar nodes in attributed networks represents a key challenge in data mining. While recent representation learning methods embed networks into low-dimensional vectors, they often implicitly assume a uniform and continuous feature space. This paper proposes a visual analytics approach using dimensionality reduction to help clarify the true topological structure of high-dimensional feature spaces formed by nodes' neighborhood attribute profiles. Analyzing inter-firm transaction networks indicates that structural roles can form complex, non-linear manifolds with density biases. Comparing this feature space with industry classifications suggested: (1) supply chain hierarchies transition continuously; (2) categories treated identically under general semantics can be clearly separated by actual transaction networks; and (3) a single industry label may fragment into multiple regions. These findings suggest potential limitations in assuming identical semantics imply similar structural roles and highlight the possible need for new similarity metrics aligned with manifold topology.

</details>


### [14] [Synthetic Contact with AI Reduces Cross-Partisan Animosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02181)
*Benjamin Lira,Noah Castelo,Stefano Puntoni,Olivier Toubia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过5项预注册研究（总N=3,960）发现，与代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人进行简短对话（合成接触）能有效降低接触门槛（相比人类外群体成员，被试愿意忍受近两倍死亡思考时间以避免人类接触）、纠正错误认知（单次10分钟对话即可将民主党员对共和党员环保态度的高估修正一个标准差以上）、改善情感（两党被试对外群体的温暖感提升）、并促进行为改变（移民议题上，实验组选择与真实跨党派人士对话的比例高出6个百分点）。然而，温暖效应在一周内大部分消退，仅有微弱残余集中在极端党派者中。对话内容分析表明，信息传递而非友善性是外群体机器人区别于对照机器人的关键因素。研究确立了合成接触作为一种可扩展、行为上有实质影响且广泛接受的跨党派互动形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去三十年美国两党间的情感温度急剧下降，但有意义的跨党派接触仍然稀缺，部分原因是人们主动回避这种接触。本研究旨在探索能否用代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人进行简短对话，来替代人们所回避的面对面接触，以缓解党派极化。

Method: 五项预注册实验（总N=3,960美国党派成员）：(1) 测量被试为避免人类外群体vs AI外群体对话愿意忍受的死亡思考时间；(2) 前-后测设计，评估单次10分钟与AI外群体机器人对话对环保态度认知偏差和情感温度的影响；(3) 三臂实验，排除纯参与效应和社交性作为驱动因素；(4) 移民议题上测量行为选择（是否愿意与真实跨党派人士对话）；(5) 追踪一周后的效应持续性。对话内容分析用于机制检验。

Result: (1) 被试为避免人类外群体对话愿意忍受的死亡思考时间几乎是对AI的两倍；(2) 基线时民主党员对共和党员环保态度的估计偏差超过一个标准差，单次对话后纠正了错误认知并提升了情感温度；(3) 效应不能由纯参与或社交性解释；(4) 移民议题上，实验组选择真实跨党派对话的比例比对照组高6个百分点；(5) 温暖效应在即时复制中显著，但一周后大部分消退，仅最极端党派者有微弱残余；内容分析显示信息传递是关键机制。

Conclusion: 合成接触是一种可扩展、行为上有实质影响且广泛接受的跨党派互动形式，但其效应持续时间有限，主要集中在短期影响上。研究为利用AI技术缓解政治极化提供了实证基础，同时指出了效应持久性方面的局限。

Abstract: Americans' warmth toward members of the opposing political party has fallen sharply over the past three decades -- yet meaningful cross-partisan contact remains scarce, in part because people actively avoid it. Across five preregistered studies (total N = 3,960 U.S. partisans), we test whether brief conversations with AI chatbots representing the political outgroup can substitute for the contact people shun. Synthetic contact first lowers the barrier to entry: partisans would endure almost twice as long contemplating their own mortality to avoid a human outgroup partner as an AI one. These conversations then correct the misperceptions that fuel division. At baseline, Democrats placed Republicans more than a standard deviation past their actual position on environmental consumption attitudes -- enough to flip the average Republican from supportive to opposed -- and a single ten-minute conversation with an outgroup chatbot corrected those beliefs and warmed affect in a within-person study of both parties. A three-arm experiment ruled out pure engagement and sociality as drivers. Synthetic contact also moved behavior, in a sample of both parties and on a more affectively charged issue: participants who spoke with an outgroup bot about immigration were six percentage points more likely than controls to choose to have a real conversation with a partisan from the other side. A final study tested whether these gains last: the warmth effect replicated immediately in a new sample; most of it faded within a week, with a small residual concentrated among the most extreme partisans. Analyzing conversation content showed that information, more than friendliness, distinguishes outgroup bots from control chatbots. Together, these findings establish synthetic contact as a scalable, behaviorally consequential, and -- unlike face-to-face contact -- widely acceptable form of cross-partisan engagement.

</details>


### [15] [What Types of Human-AI Teams Exist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02198)
*Nathan Hughes,Ibrahim Habli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析53篇人-AI团队文献，基于心理学团队分类法将其分为5类（AI助手、临时依赖、临时强制依赖、配对平等、群体平等），指出不同研究对"人-AI团队"的定义存在差异，导致结论难以迁移，并提出了分类识别指南和报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-AI团队研究跨多个领域，但不同研究对团队类型的定义模糊且不一致，导致难以理解各类团队之间的异同，以及研究成果是否具有可迁移性。

Method: 系统分析53篇人-AI团队相关论文，基于心理学团队分类法进行聚类分析，识别出五种团队类型。

Result: 发现53篇论文可归为5个聚类：AI助手型、临时依赖型、临时强制依赖型、配对平等型、群体平等型。每种类型具有独特的整体性团队特征，表明许多研究虽使用同一术语但研究的是不同类型的人-AI团队。

Conclusion: 当前人-AI团队研究存在定义不统一的问题，导致研究结论难以跨类型迁移；提出了人-AI团队类型的识别指南、研究报告清单以及促进该领域综合发展的方法。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming has received increasing attention in the literature. However, the range of studies conducted in multiple domains make it difficult to understand what types of teams are being studied, and in what ways are they similar/different from one another. In this study, we analyse 53 papers on human-AI teams and categorise them into five main clusters based on psychological taxonomies of teaming; AI Assistant, Ad-hoc Dependency, Ad-hoc Forced Dependency, Paired Equanimity, and Group Equanimity. Each cluster represents a unique combination of holistic team-level characteristics, indicating there are multiple disparate team types studied under the same definition. In turn, this raises the question of whether insights are truly transferable between papers. We conclude with guidance on how to identify the types of human-AI teams studied, a checklist for reporting a human-AI team in research work, and ways in which the field can be further synthesised.

</details>


### [16] [Personality Without Persons? A Psychometric Critique of Big Five Testing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02325)
*Kim Zierahn,Cristina Cachero,Anna Korhonen,Nuria Oliver*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大五人格量表在LLMs中的心理测量学适用性，发现这些量表无法有效描述LLMs、无法捕捉模型间差异，且无法复现大五人格的五因子结构，表明直接套用人类人格框架会产生误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大五人格量表被广泛用于表征和比较LLMs，但这些量表是为人类开发和验证的，其在LLMs中的适用性未经系统检验，可能导致对LLMs的误导性描述和治理决策。

Method: 系统评估了五个候选大五人格量表的内容效度，选择最优量表对244个不同模型（涵盖49个模型家族）进行施测，分析得分变异性、因子结构及对齐训练对得分的影响。

Result: 1) 为LLMs改编的项目可达足够内容效度，但原始人类项目不行；2) 模型间变异仅占总方差3%，量表无法捕捉有意义差异；3) LLM响应未能复现五因子结构，其中四个因子高度相关（r>=0.92）合并为一个因子；4) 指令微调（对齐训练）系统性地将得分向社会期望特质偏移。

Conclusion: 大五人格得分在LLMs中并未测量到与人类人格等价的构念，直接套用人类人格框架会产生误导性表征，需开发专门针对LLMs的评估框架。

Abstract: Human personality inventories are increasingly used to characterize large language models (LLMs), compare systems, and inform downstream governance claims. Yet, these inventories were developed and validated for humans, and it remains unclear whether they apply to LLMs. We present a systematic psychometric evaluation of Big Five personality measurements in LLMs. We ask three research questions: Do Big Five inventories a) appropriately describe LLMs, b) capture inter-individual differences across models, and c) reflect internal factors consistent with human personality. We assess content validity of five candidate Big Five inventories and administer the winning inventory to N = 244 different models spanning 49 model families. First, we found that Big Five items adapted for LLMs can reach sufficient content validity, while original human-developed items did not. Second, Big Five inventories did not capture meaningful differences between LLMs: We found low variability between models, accounting for only 3% of total score variance. Third, LLMs responses did not recover the Big Five five-factor structure with four of the Big Five facets collapsing into one (r >= .92). Direct comparisons between base and instruction-tuned model variants suggested that alignment training systematically shifted Big Five scores toward socially desirable traits. These findings demonstrate that Big Five scores do not measure a construct equivalent to human personality in LLMs. Applying human personality frameworks to LLMs produces misleading characterizations used to benchmark, compare, and govern LLMs. We highlight the need for evaluation frameworks that are developed for LLMs, rather than adopting human constructs without validation.

</details>


### [17] [Physical surfaces make touch interactions in virtual reality precise, efficient, and bimanual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02430)
*Wen Ying,Seongkook Heo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了虚拟现实中不同类型的手部接地触觉反馈（无反馈、触觉反馈、物理表面）对高精度触摸任务（选择、追踪、素描）的影响，发现便携式物理表面在精度、效率和质量上表现最佳，且提高了双手协作和用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统中的空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参考表面，导致高精度触摸交互困难，本文旨在探究不同类型的手部接地触觉反馈如何影响VR任务的触摸表现。

Method: 实验比较了三种触觉反馈条件：无触觉反馈（仅视觉）、触觉反馈（振动/压力）、物理表面（便携式可触表面），让用户执行选择、追踪和素描等任务。

Result: 物理表面在选择的精度、追踪的效率和素描的质量上最优；使用物理表面时用户的双手协作利用率提高；用户偏好物理表面交互，归因于更强的信心和控制感。

Conclusion: 为高精度VR触摸任务提供便携式物理表面作为接地参考，显著优于仅靠触觉或视觉反馈，能提升性能与用户体验。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) systems can enable convenient hand-based interactions across diverse work scenarios. However, mid-air gestures lack tactile feedback and a physical reference surface to support the hand. This absence of haptic grounding can cause significant challenges in achieving precise and efficient touch interactions. This paper investigates the effect of different types of hand-grounded haptic feedback on the touch performance of VR tasks that demand high precision, such as selecting, tracing, and sketching. We compared three levels of haptic feedback: 1) No Haptic Feedback, where only visual feedback was provided; 2) Tactile Feedback, where users received vibrotactile and pressure feedback upon touching a virtual surface; 3) Physical Surface, where users interacted with a portable and tangible surface.
  Our study found that portable physical surfaces enabled the best selection precision, tracing efficiency, and sketch quality. Furthermore, participants showed increased bimanual hand utilization when engaging with a physical surface during tasks. These observed behaviors corresponded to participants' preference for interacting with physical surfaces, attributed to a better sense of confidence and control.

</details>


### [18] [When Do LLM Personas Support Visualization Design? A Cross-Model Study of Color Assignment and Chart Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02455)
*Shahreen Salim,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于大语言模型人格角色在可视化设计中的稳定性，发现人格-颜色关联高度依赖模型配置，而图表偏好中任务情境比人格更主导首选项，建议将LLM角色作为探索性工具而非人类替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM人格角色在可视化设计任务中反映的是稳定的人格效应，还是模型选择与任务框架的人为产物。

Method: 使用43个Big Five人格剖面，在GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4.1-mini、GPT-5-mini三个模型上，进行颜色分配（抽象/具体概念）和图表习语偏好评分两项可视化相关任务，并引入无人格基线对照。

Result: 人格-颜色耦合高度依赖模型配置（GPT-4o-mini无关联，GPT-4.1-mini全部一致，GPT-5-mini部分一致）；抽象概念中人格解释的色相方差高于模型身份；图表偏好中任务情境比人格更主导首选项（无人格基线在9个模型-情境组合中恢复出8个相同的首选）。

Conclusion: LLM人格角色应作为可视化设计的探索性探针而非人类被试的替代品，未来研究需进行多模型测试、概念类型分解和无人格基线对照。

Abstract: Large language model personas are increasingly used to approximate diverse users during early-stage visualization design, but it remains unclear whether persona-conditioned outputs reflect stable personality effects or artifacts of model choice and task framing. We examine this question across two visualization-relevant tasks: color assignment for abstract and concrete concepts, and chart-idiom preference ratings across task contexts. Using 43 Big Five profiles across GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4.1-mini, and GPT-5-mini, we find that personality-color coupling is highly model-configuration dependent: absent in GPT-4o-mini for all six concepts, consistent in GPT-4.1-mini across all six, and partial in GPT-5-mini for two of six. Concept type further shapes the signal: for abstract concepts, personality explains more hue variance than model identity, while concrete concepts show smaller and comparable effects. In chart choice, trait-aligned cluster aggregation produces stable top-idiom rankings across all nine cluster-context combinations, but a no-persona baseline recovers the same top choice in 8 of 9 model-context cells, indicating that task context drives rank-1 selection more than personality. These findings position LLM personas as exploratory probes for visualization design, not substitutes for human participants, and motivate multi-model testing, concept-type disaggregation, and no-persona baselines in future studies.

</details>

### [19] [Embedding Inference Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01276)
*Cedric Fitiavana Raelijohn,Sébastien Gambs,Jean-Francois Rajotte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究黑盒信息检索系统中的嵌入推断攻击（EIA），攻击者仅通过观察检索到的文档集合，即可识别系统背后使用的嵌入模型，并在包含重排序器或RAG系统的场景下验证了攻击的有效性，同时提出了相似度阈值等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型反演攻击通常需要攻击者知道嵌入模型本身（白盒或半黑盒），但在真实IR系统中，嵌入模型通常隐藏于API之后，攻击者仅能观察到检索结果。本文旨在探索更严苛的黑盒场景下（仅知检索到的文档集合，无排序或相似度分数），是否仍能识别出后端使用的嵌入模型，从而揭示更隐蔽的安全威胁。

Method: 1. 定义嵌入推断攻击（EIA）问题：攻击者从候选模型集合中，通过构造特定查询并观察返回的无序文档集合来识别模型。2. 设计具有判别性的查询构造方法，使得不同嵌入模型对同一查询的检索结果差异最大化。3. 在包含重排序器的IR系统中验证攻击有效性。4. 在真实RAG系统中测试攻击，并处理LLM对非标准输入的拒答问题。5. 提出相似度阈值等防御策略并评估其效果。

Result: 1. 在黑盒场景下（仅观察无序文档集合），EIA攻击能够有效识别目标嵌入模型。2. 即使系统包含重排序器，某些查询仍保持判别性。3. 在真实RAG系统中，精心构造的查询能够绕过LLM的输入检查，成功发起攻击。4. 提出的相似度阈值等缓解策略可以部分防御该攻击。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了黑盒IR系统中一种新的安全威胁——嵌入推断攻击，证明了仅凭检索结果即可推断后端嵌入模型，并指出该攻击在包含重排序器或RAG系统的实际场景中依然有效，同时提供了初步的防御方向。

Abstract: Embedding models are essential components of modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems, yet they are typically hidden behind APIs. Recent works have shown that dense IR system can lead to security vulnerabilities such as embedding inversion attacks. However, such attacks usually require that the attacker knows the embedding model for the attack to be applicable. In this paper, we study IR systems under a black-box setting in which the adversary observes only the unordered set of retrieved documents, without ranking or similarity scores. We demonstrate that in such contexts, tailored queries allow an adversary to identify which embedding model is in use from a set of known model candidate, which we coin as an embedding inference attack (EIA). We also show that certain queries remain discriminative even when the system includes a reranker as a potential defense mechanism. We further validate our method on a real Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system, in which the tailored queries bypass the LLM's tendency to reject inputs it does not recognize as well-formed questions. Finally, we propose and evaluate other mitigation strategies such as similarity thresholds.

</details>


### [20] [Chameleon: Recovering Cyber-Physical Systems from Memory Corruption Attacks via ML Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01356)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chameleon是一个利用机器学习代理自动恢复受内存破坏攻击的CPS的框架，在攻击检测时用训练好的代理替换受损组件，实验证明其在七种机器人平台上有效恢复系统并完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS安全技术面对攻击时采取终止、停机或降级处理，这对安全关键型任务不可接受，需要一种能在攻击发生后自动恢复系统正常功能的方法。

Method: 基于LLVM编译器实现，以组件为粒度训练机器学习代理模型，使其近似模拟原始组件行为但不携带相同内存破坏漏洞；攻击检测后自动将受损组件替换为代理。

Result: 在7种机器人平台上评估，代理模型平均R²=0.96，能成功从真实世界内存破坏攻击中恢复系统并完成任务，且性能和内存开销低。

Conclusion: Chameleon提供了一种可行的CPS攻击后自动恢复方案，优于现有终止或降级方法，适用于安全关键型CPS场景。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly deployed in every aspect of our lives and can be compromised through memory corruption vulnerabilities, allowing attackers to hijack the control flow and take over the system. Existing techniques mostly focus on detecting such attacks but respond by terminating or halting execution upon attack detection, which is not acceptable in CPSs used in safety-critical tasks, as interrupted tasks can have catastrophic consequences. Other techniques replace compromised CPS components with simplified defaults that degrade system behavior, or reboot the system upon attack detection.
  We propose Chameleon, a novel framework for automatically recovering CPSs from memory corruption attacks using machine learning (ML)-based surrogates trained at compartment granularity that nearly replicate their original compartments' behavior but do not have the same memory corruption vulnerabilities. Upon attack detection, Chameleon replaces the compromised compartment with its trained surrogate. We implemented Chameleon using the LLVM compiler and evaluated its efficiency and effectiveness on seven different robotic vehicles (RVs), including simulated and real ones. We found that Chameleon can generate surrogates that closely approximate the original compartments (with an average R$^2$=0.96), successfully recover the system despite real-world memory corruption attacks unlike prior approaches, and complete their tasks while incurring low performance and memory overhead.

</details>


### [21] [Cognitive Firewall: A Proactive, Zero-Trust, Multi-Gate Framework for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01277)
*Michele Guida,Ruslan Shikhhamzayev,Sindhuja Penchala,Stefano Iannucci,Jiacheng Li,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognitive Firewall，一种在用户与目标LLM之间插入独立监督模型的主动式运行时安全框架，通过四个分类门（意图门、零信任上下文门、一致性门、输出风险门）对对话进行分解式安全评估，采用升级机制而非分数平均进行决策。实验表明该方法在多个越狱基准上大幅降低攻击成功率（最低降至2%），同时保持8%的过度拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全机制通常将提示或响应作为孤立消息进行审查，无法恢复累积意图、验证声称的权限或检测跨轮分解的有害目标，难以应对多轮对话中的渐进式攻击。

Method: 将安全评估分解为四个分类门：意图门（识别请求的操作目标）、零信任上下文门（将声称的角色和权限视为未经验证证据）、一致性门（检测跨轮的升级和分解）、输出风险门（在释放前检查候选响应）。门决策通过升级机制而非分数平均进行组合，任一门的危险信号即可阻断交互。

Result: 在四个越狱基准和一个良性安全测试集上，Cognitive Firewall将攻击成功率降至2%以下（三个攻击集）和14%（最难的人工构建攻击集），同时保持8%的过度拒绝率。

Conclusion: 分解式、对话级别的监督能够改善LLM安全的主动式防护和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be induced to produce harmful content through multi turn strategies in which no single user message appears clearly unsafe. Existing runtime safeguards commonly evaluate prompts or responses as isolated messages, which limits their ability to recover ac-cumulated intent, verify asserted authority, or detect harmful objectives decomposed across a dialogue. This paper presents the Cognitive Firewall, a proactive runtime oversight framework that interposes an independent oversight model between a user and a protected target mod l. The framework decomposes safety assessment into four categorical gates: an intent gate that identi-fies the operational objective of a request, a zero trust context gate that treats claimed roles and permissions as unverified evidence, a consistency gate that detects escalation and decomposition across turns, and an output risk gate that inspects candidate responses before release. Gate decisions are combined through escalation rather than score averaging, allowing any confident danger signal to block an interaction while preserving an auditable rationale. Experiments on four jailbreak benchmarks and a benign safety test set show that the Cognitive Firewall substantially reduces attack success across single turn, multi turn, authority based, and human crafted attacks. It lowers attack success to 2 percent or below on three attack sets and to 14 percent on the most difficult human crafted set, while maintaining an 8 percent over refusal rate. These results indicate that decomposed, conversation level oversight can improve proactive containment and auditability for LLM safety.

</details>


### [22] [Generative AI and Federated Learning for Intrusion Detection Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01305)
*Jiefei Liu,Abu Saleh Md Tayeen,Pratyay Kumar,Qixu Gong,Wenbin Jiang,Huiping Cao,Satyajayant Misra,Jayashree Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了生成式AI与联邦学习在网络入侵检测系统中的应用，涵盖生成模型（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）在异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强等任务中的作用，以及FL在分布式隐私保护训练中的优势，并讨论了合成数据质量、对抗风险、非IID分布等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDS模型面临攻击行为演变、真实数据集难以获取、流量记录不完整、类别不平衡以及隐私约束限制集中式数据收集等挑战，而生成式AI和联邦学习为解决这些问题提供了新机遇，亟需系统性综述以梳理该交叉领域的研究现状与未来方向。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，首先总结IDS主要研究方向（对抗机器学习、异常检测、物联网IDS、可解释IDS及基准数据集）；然后按模型家族和任务目标对生成式AI在IDS中的应用进行分类（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）；最后综述生成式AI与FL结合的IDS研究，并分析开放挑战。

Result: 归纳了生成式AI在IDS中的多种应用场景：异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强、数据填补、对抗流量生成和IDS告警解释；总结了FL支持分布式IDS训练、实现隐私保护的机制；识别了包括合成数据质量、真实流量生成、双用途对抗风险、非IID客户端分布、通信高效模型共享、联邦IDS基准测试和网络安全领域专用LLM等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 生成式AI与FL的结合为IDS提供了解决数据稀缺、类别不平衡和隐私约束的有效途径，但合成数据的真实性保障、对抗攻击的双刃剑效应、非IID数据分布下的联邦训练效率以及领域专用LLM的构建仍是未来研究的重点方向。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for monitoring network traffic and identifying malicious activities in modern cyber-physical, Internet of Things (IoT), enterprise, and distributed network environments. However, developing reliable IDS models remains challenging because attack behaviors evolve over time, realistic datasets are difficult to obtain, traffic records may be incomplete, attack classes are often imbalanced, and privacy constraints limit centralized data collection. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and Federated Learning (FL) provide new opportunities to address these limitations. Generative models can support anomaly detection, synthetic traffic generation, data augmentation, data imputation, adversarial traffic generation, and IDS alert explanation. FL enables distributed IDS training without directly sharing local network traffic, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive and geographically distributed environments. This survey provides a structured review of generative AI and FL techniques for IDS. We first summarize representative IDS research directions, including adversarial machine learning, anomaly-based detection, IoT-oriented IDS, explainable IDS, and benchmark datasets. We then categorize generative AI applications in IDS according to model families and task objectives, covering autoencoder-based models, Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), diffusion models, and Large Language Models (LLMs). Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

</details>


### [23] [An alternative approach towards attacks against fully-split PLWE instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01340)
*Iván Blanco-Chacón,Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma,Raúl Durán Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于根的密码攻击的泛化问题，通过构建显式同构证明了在完全分裂设定下，此类攻击无法产生新的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨根攻击能否通过构造显式同构扩展到其他实例，从而发现新的安全漏洞。

Method: 首先构造了完全分裂多项式环与已有攻击适用的多项式环之间的显式同构，证明该同构会扭曲样本使其不可区分；继而证明完全分裂多项式环的任何同构都必须具有该构造形式。

Result: 在完全分裂设定下，通过构造显式同构扩展根攻击无法产生新的漏洞。

Conclusion: 完全分裂设定下根攻击的泛化途径是无效的，形式化证明了该方向的安全性。

Abstract: In the present work we address some key questions regarding the generalization of root-based attacks presented in a recent work by the authors. In particular, we analyze potential root-based attacks extensions via the construction of explicit isomorphisms from vulnerable instances, and provide a formal proof that this approach will not yield any new vulnerabilities under a fully-split setting. To do so, we first construct an explicit isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings and polynomial rings where previous attacks apply and show that the application of such an isomorphism will always distort the samples in a way that the resulting samples cannot be used to distinguish. Then, we prove that any isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings must be of the form of the constructed isomorphism.

</details>


### [24] [From Forgeries to Foundation Models: A Systematic Survey of Identity Document Attack and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01442)
*Gourab Das,Pavan Kumar C,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次统一了针对身份伪造的威胁模型，涵盖物理呈现攻击、数字注入攻击和生成式AI合成攻击，系统回顾了从规则启发式到基础模型的检测方法，揭示了现有基准与现实部署之间的持续性差距，并指出即使最强模型在零样本测试中APCER仍高于25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具使得高保真文档合成和字段级篡改变得容易，但现有检测方法受限于不能反映这一威胁的基准测试，需要一个统一的威胁模型和评估框架来应对物理呈现、数字注入和完全生成式合成等多种攻击面。

Method: 采用统一的身份验证威胁模型对Presentation Attacks、Digital Injection Attacks和GenAI驱动合成进行整体处理；系统追溯检测方法从规则启发式、取证定位、注入感知流水线、基础模型到少样本框架的演进；对2019-2025年公共数据集进行系统性审计；分析大型多模态模型在身份证件操作中的表现，识别出脚本依赖生成不稳定性（SDGI）；在未见过的合成身份证上进行零样本基准测试。

Result: 发现基准条件与操作部署之间存在持续的现实差距（Reality Gap）；识别出非拉丁文字幕修复中的重复性排版故障模式SDGI；零样本测试中即使最强公开模型在安全导向操作条件下APCER仍高于25%，表明跨领域泛化能力存在显著局限。

Conclusion: 未来应朝着取证导向、隐私保护和法律可问责的身份验证系统方向发展。

Abstract: Identity document forgery has undergone a fundamental capability shift: generative AI tools now enable high-fidelity document synthesis and field-level manipulation with minimal technical expertise, while detection methods remain constrained by benchmarks that do not reflect this threat. The resulting attack surface spans physical presentation, digital injection, and fully generative synthesis, introducing distinct forensic failure modes that require a unified threat model and evaluation framework. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified treatment of Presentation Attacks, Digital Injection Attacks, and GenAI-driven synthesis within a single identity verification threat model. We trace detection methodologies from rule-based heuristics through forensic localisation, injection-aware pipelines, foundation models, and few-shot frameworks. A systematic audit of public datasets from 2019--2025 exposes a persistent Reality Gap between benchmark conditions and operational deployment. We further analyse large multimodal models for identity document manipulation, identifying Script-Dependent Generative Instability (SDGI) as a recurring typographic failure mode in non-Latin script inpainting. Finally, zero-shot benchmarking on unseen synthesised ID cards shows that even the strongest publicly available models achieve APCER values above 25% under security-oriented operating conditions, highlighting substantial limits in cross-domain generalisation. We conclude by outlining future directions toward forensically grounded, privacy-preserving, and legally accountable identity verification systems.

</details>


### [25] [Hamm-Grams: An Algorithm for Mining Regular Expressions of Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01445)
*Derek Everett,Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出hamm-grams特征（含通配符的正则表达式）替代传统n-grams，通过局部敏感哈希和聚类算法高效构建，在恶意软件分类检测中表现更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统n-grams特征在恶意软件检测中虽然有效，但其本质上是脆弱的（brittle），容易因微小扰动而失效，需要构建更鲁棒的特征。

Method: 提出hamm-grams特征（固定长度含单字符通配符的正则表达式）；设计新的局部敏感哈希（LSH）使小汉明距离的样本碰撞；通过哈希桶内聚类确定通配符位置。

Result: 在恶意软件分类和检测任务中验证了hamm-grams相比n-grams的优势。

Conclusion: hamm-grams作为一种更鲁棒的静态特征，能有效提升恶意软件检测系统的性能。

Abstract: Malware poses a critical and ever-evolving threat, and robust and effective systems for detecting and classifying malware are of essential importance. $n$-grams features are among the common static features used in effective machine learning systems for malware, but these features are inherently brittle. We propose an algorithm for constructing more robust features, hamm-grams, which are a special class of regular expressions having a fixed length and single-character wildcards. We devise an efficient algorithm for finding common hamm-grams using a new locality-sensitive hash designed to produce collisions among pairs of small Hamming distance and a clustering within hash buckets to place wildcards. We then demonstrate the advantages of these features in malware classification and detection tasks.

</details>


### [26] [Overthink-Triggered Slowdown Attacks on LVLM-Based Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01518)
*Qiang Han,Jie Wu,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)在机器人系统中的"过度思考"行为（生成过长推理链导致推理延迟），并提出了一种对抗性攻击方法：通过在视觉场景中嵌入精心设计的、人类可读的场景文本作为触发器，在严格黑盒设置下显著拖慢受害者机器人系统的决策速度。作者提出了三阶段框架来系统性识别和验证可迁移的过度思考触发器，在三种代表性LVLM上实现了最高6.96x的延迟放大倍数，物理打印文本触发器仍能达到4.74x的延迟放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在机器人系统中应用时存在过度思考行为，即生成过长的推理链导致推理时间过长。这种行为存在严重安全风险：攻击者可以通过刻意触发过度思考来拖慢受害者机器人系统的决策速度，导致一系列安全问题（称为"过度思考引发的减速攻击"）。现有研究尚未系统性地识别和验证这种攻击的触发因素及其跨模型可迁移性。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) 构建推理密集型场景文本语料库，从短响应前缀中提取与过度思考相关的词汇特征；2) 基于前缀代理分数进行高效黑盒搜索，对少量候选者通过完全延迟测量进行选择性确认；3) 使用固定触发器池在未见图像和多个LVLMs上评估黑盒迁移性，报告延迟放大倍数和攻击成功率。

Result: 在三种代表性LVLMs上，所有触发器均产生大于1.0x的减速比，最强单触发器案例达到6.96x延迟放大。物理打印文本触发器仍能导致高达4.74x的延迟放大。实验证明发现的触发器在多个LVLM模型间具有可迁移性，能够一致地导致机器人系统显著减速。

Conclusion: 本研究系统性地证明了针对LVLM机器人系统的过度思考诱导攻击的有效性和跨模型可迁移性，揭示了嵌入场景文本作为攻击触发器的实际可行性，即使是物理打印形式也能造成显著影响。这项工作为LVLM在机器人系统中部署的安全性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have been increasingly integrated into robotic systems. However, these models may exhibit overthinking behaviors, where they generate excessively long reasoning traces, incurring an excessive inference time. This overthinking behavior poses a serious risk to robotic systems, as the adversary can deliberately trigger overthinking to slow down the decision making of a victim robotic system, causing a variety of safety issues (i.e., an overthinking-induced slowdown attack). To initiate this attack, an adversary can embed carefully crafted, human-readable scene text into the visual scene observed by a victim robotic agent, causing significant inference delays even under a strict black-box setting. Therefore, the embedded scene text serves as a significant "trigger" for the attack.
  This work systematically identifies and validates transferable triggers of overthinking in robotic systems by introducing a three-stage framework. First, we construct a diverse corpus of reasoning-intensive scene text and extract overthinking-correlated lexical features from short response prefixes. Second, we perform an efficient black-box search guided by a prefix-based proxy score while selectively confirming a small set of top candidates with full latency measurements. Third, we evaluate black-box transfer using a fixed pool of triggers on unseen images and multiple LVLMs, reporting latency amplification and attack success rates under standard thresholds. Across three representative LVLMs, all triggers yield slowdown ratios greater than 1.0x, with the strongest single-trigger case reaching 6.96x. The physical printing of the text trigger still causes up to 4.74x latency amplification. These results demonstrate that our discovered triggers are transferred between multiple LVLM models and consistently cause significant slowdowns in robotic systems.

</details>


### [27] [LIB-TRAP: Standard Cell Library Hardware Trojan Risk Assessment and Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01526)
*Harish Kumar Dharavath,Md Muhtasim Alam Chowdhury,Rozhin Yasaei,Soheil Salehi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型硬件木马威胁模型，即标准单元库本身被恶意篡改。设计方使用被篡改的库进行综合，而代工厂在制造时将库中休眠的木马单元替换为激活版本。通过在Saed32nm和Sky130nm工艺上构建恶意库，并在AES-128、以太网控制器和WISHBONE DMA三个基准电路上验证，该攻击向量能够有效隐藏木马，且难以通过面积、功耗等设计级特征检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件木马研究主要关注标准单元之间的空间，但缺乏对构成绝大多数数字设计基础的标准单元本身脆弱性的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索标准单元不可信情况下的新型威胁模型。

Method: 将标准单元库转换为恶意库：库中包含休眠态（deactivated）和激活态（activated）两种木马单元。设计方使用休眠态库进行综合与实现，代工厂在制造时替换为激活态单元。使用开源和工业标准EDA工具，基于Saed32nm和Sky130nm工艺构建恶意库，并在多个基准设计上综合验证。

Result: 在Synopsys 32nm和SkyWater 130nm工艺下，对AES-128加密核、以太网控制器和WISHBONE DMA引擎三个基准电路进行了综合。提取了总单元数、总面积、动态功耗和静态功耗等设计级特征，用于二元分类分析。

Conclusion: 基于标准单元库篡改的硬件木马攻击向量具有有效性和隐蔽性，能够绕过基于设计级特征的检测方法，对硬件安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities inherent to the fabless semiconductor manufacturing model have significantly increased the risk of malicious Hardware Trojan (HT) insertion, posing severe threats to hardware security. Several HT mitigation and detection strategies have been developed, and existing works explore the insertion of HTs in the space between standard cells in an integrated circuit. However, there is a lack of research into the vulnerabilities posed by the building blocks of most digital designs on the market today, the standard cells. This work investigates a novel threat model in which standard cells are considered untrusted. Our proposed threat model provides the design house with a tampered standard cell library. The intended netlist is synthesized and implemented using the tampered library. During fabrication, a nefarious foundry replaces the library's deactivated HT cells with activated counterparts. Using open-source and industry-standard Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tools, existing standard cell libraries, Saed32nm and Sky130nm, are converted into malicious libraries capable of masking the presence of arbitrary HTs from IC designers. The malicious library is then applied and characterized in multiple standard benchmark designs. To demonstrate the efficacy and stealthiness of this standard cell-based attack vector, three benchmark circuits, an AES-128 encryption core, an Ethernet controller, and a WISHBONE DMA engine, were synthesized using both clean and Trojan-infected libraries across Synopsys 32nm and SkyWater 130nm technologies. Design-level features, including total cell count, total area, dynamic power consumption, and static power, were extracted from these synthesized circuits to serve as inputs for binary classification

</details>


### [28] [VeriChat: An Agentic Conversational AI Assistant for Hardware Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01668)
*Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Shams Tarek,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriChat是一个领域专用的对话助手，采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，为硬件安全验证提供上下文感知的指导，并通过集成开源EDA工具实现RTL设计的语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证，在可信度评分上达到87.73%，显著优于闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全验证过程复杂，工程师需要面对设计分析、威胁考量和验证策略等多重挑战，而现有验证环境缺乏结构化的安全指导支持。通用聊天机器人（如ChatGPT、Gemini）存在幻觉问题和静态过时知识的局限，无法可靠地用于硬件安全验证场景。

Method: VeriChat采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，包含三个专门化智能体协同工作以最小化幻觉并提高响应的透明度和可靠性。系统集成了开源EDA工具（Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys），可直接在用户提供的RTL设计上执行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证。

Result: 在综合评估方法下，VeriChat的Faithfulness得分为87.73%，显著优于领先的专有模型。通过AES S-Box IP上的硬件木马检测案例研究，验证了系统能够自主识别、仿真并形式化证明隐藏的密钥泄露漏洞。

Conclusion: VeriChat成功展示了领域专用对话助手在硬件安全验证中的实用价值，通过多智能体协作和EDA工具集成，提供了比通用大模型更可靠、更透明的安全验证支持。

Abstract: Hardware security verification is a multi-stage process in which engineers must navigate complex design analyses, threat considerations, and verification strategies. They often need security-focused guidance, yet current verification environments provide little structured support for such assistance. Although conversational AI could offer such on-demand assistance, directly using general-purpose chatbots like ChatGPT or Gemini is risky due to their tendency to hallucinate and their reliance on static, outdated knowledge. We present VeriChat, a domain-specialized conversational assistant designed to support, rather than replace, existing verification workflows by providing context-aware security guidance. VeriChat employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent workflow in which three specialized agents collaboratively minimize hallucinations while improving the transparency and reliability of the response. Beyond question answering, VeriChat integrates open-source EDA tools, including Icarus Verilog, Yosys, and SymbiYosys, to perform syntax checking, synthesis analysis, simulation, and formal verification directly on user-provided RTL designs. Evaluated using a comprehensive methodology, VeriChat achieves a Faithfulness score of 87.73%, significantly outperforming the leading proprietary models. We demonstrate the framework through a hardware Trojan detection case study on an AES S-Box IP, where VeriChat autonomously identifies, simulates, and formally proves a covert key-leakage vulnerability through a multi-turn conversational workflow.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Gradient-Based Attacks: Adversarial Robustness and Explainability Stability in Cybersecurity Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01679)
*Mona Rajhans,Vishal Khawarey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗攻击对网络安全分类器及其SHAP解释稳定性的影响。针对随机森林和XGBoost，在四个表格安全数据集上评估五种攻击方法（含三种黑盒方法）。提出可解释性稳定性指数（ESI），与鲁棒性指数（RI）共同量化攻击影响。发现梯度攻击在XGBoost上产生退化结果（RI≈0.98），而得分攻击暴露真实脆弱性（RI≈0.36）。退化扰动仍导致显著归因漂移（XGBoost ESI=0.06-0.16），表明预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是需联合测量的不同维度。提出两轴框架（梯度依赖、查询效率）解释攻击排序，并通过步长消融实验解释PGD在z-score标准化数据上的异常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗攻击对网络安全分类器构成双重威胁：不仅降低预测性能，还会破坏安全分析师依赖的SHAP解释稳定性。现有研究缺乏同时对预测鲁棒性和解释稳定性进行联合评估的框架，尤其是针对树模型这类非可微模型。

Method: 扩展前期MLP研究至RF和XGBoost，使用四个表格安全数据集，评估五种攻击方法（含三种黑盒方法）。提出ESI指标，与RI在同一[0,1]尺度上报告。采用两轴框架（梯度依赖、查询效率）解释攻击排序，并进行步长消融实验。

Result: 梯度攻击(ZOO)在XGBoost上因分段常数预测面产生退化结果(RI≈0.98)，得分攻击暴露真实脆弱性(RI≈0.36)。退化扰动仍导致显著归因漂移：XGBoost ESI=0.06-0.16（尽管ZOO鲁棒性近乎完美），RF ESI=0.14-0.29。两轴框架有效解释攻击排序，步长消融解释PGD在z-score数据上的异常。

Conclusion: 预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是不同维度，需要联合测量。提出的ESI指标和两轴框架为树集成模型的安全评估提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on cybersecurity classifiers pose a dual threat: degrading predictions and destabilising the SHAP-based explanations that security analysts rely on to understand and triage alerts. We extend our prior MLP conference study to Random Forest and XGBoost across four tabular security datasets (phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021), evaluating five attacks including three black-box methods applicable to non-differentiable tree models. We introduce the Explainability Stability Index (ESI), a scalar metric computed from TreeSHAP attribution drift under adversarial perturbation, reported on the same [0,1] scale as the Robustness Index (RI). A key finding is that gradient-based black-box attacks (ZOO) produce degenerate results against XGBoost (apparent RI ~0.98) due to piecewise-constant prediction surfaces, while score-based Square Attack reveals genuine vulnerability (RI ~0.36). These degenerate perturbations still drive substantial attribution drift: XGBoost ESI ~0.06-0.16 despite near-perfect ZOO robustness, versus 0.14-0.29 for RF, showing that prediction robustness and explanation stability are distinct axes requiring joint measurement. A two-axis framework (gradient dependence, query efficiency) explains the observed attack ranking and yields practical guidance for tree ensemble evaluation. A step-size ablation explains a counterintuitive PGD anomaly on z-score normalised tabular data.

</details>


### [30] [Pmeta-TLA: Backdoor Attacks for Speech Classification Models via Meta-Learning with Timbre Leakage Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01702)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA)的语音后门攻击方法，通过在深度自监督特征中传播音色信息制作隐蔽性强的中毒样本，并设计了Pmeta-TLA机制实现一次性嵌入多个后门，在关键词识别任务上实现了高攻击效能、强隐蔽性、鲁棒性和低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音后门攻击触发器容易被深度神经网络防御器检测，亟需探索更隐蔽的攻击技术以揭示并防范此类安全威胁。

Method: 提出TLA攻击，在帧级别将音色信息传播到深度自监督特征中，生成自然感知的中毒样本；进一步提出Pmeta-TLA，利用元学习和Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad)实现一次性多后门注入训练策略，并将TLA作为多目标攻击工具。

Result: 在关键词识别任务上使用多种深度神经网络模型进行数据中毒后门攻击实验，结果表明所提策略在攻击效能、隐蔽性、鲁棒性和攻击成本方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TLA和Pmeta-TLA能有效突破现有防御机制，为语音后门攻击安全研究提供了新的视角和防范依据。

Abstract: Recently, speech classification methods have gained widespread adoption in intelligent gadgets. Current study indicates that backdoor attacks provide a substantial security concern to these models, underscoring the pressing necessity to investigate additional potential attack techniques to expose and prevent such risks. This work discusses the vulnerability of current speech triggers to detection by deep neural network defenders and introduces the Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA). The suggested trigger disseminates timbre information at the frame level within the deep self-supervised features, producing poisoned samples that appear natural to human perception. Furthermore, we introduce Pmeta-TLA, an innovative training mechanism for embedding numerous backdoors one time. This method proposes a multi-backdoor injection training strategy using meta-learning and Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad) and introduces TLA as a multi-target attack tool within it. We performed tests on data-poisoning backdoor attacks in keyword spotting tasks utilizing some deep neural network models. Experimental results indicate that the proposed strategy attains superior Attack efficacy, enhanced stealthiness, robustness, and a reduced attack cost relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [31] [Trust Boundary Semantic Gaps: A Multi-dimensional Analysis and Mitigation for Security-by-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01711)
*Doyeon Kim,Jin-Young Choi,Junghee Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出信任边界语义鸿沟（TBSG）概念，指代工件跨信任边界时通过正确的语法验证但未能满足接收域语义安全需求的情况。基于75起安全事件（2014-2025）分析，构建四维分析模型（MDTBSG：身份、空间、时间、解释），并提出设计阶段框架TBSAM，用于识别、优先级划分、追溯和缓解TBSG，通过SolarWinds/SUNBURST案例验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于格式、协议和签名的语法验证机制虽然必要，但无法确保工件满足接收域所需的语义安全属性，导致已签名更新或已认证令牌虽通过验证仍可能造成系统被攻破。这种"语法通过但语义不足"的鸿沟缺乏系统性的认知和分析方法。

Method: （1）分析75起公开安全事件（2014-2025），在边界层面归纳语义错配；（2）构建四维分析模型MDTBSG：身份（Identity）、空间（Spatial）、时间（Temporal）、解释（Interpretation）；（3）提出设计阶段框架TBSAM，从设计规约中识别TBSG、优先级排序、追踪传播至原始边界、映射到候选架构控制措施；（4）通过SolarWinds/SUNBURST供应链攻击进行回顾性重构验证。

Result: TBSAM能够显式化接收域的隐式假设，区分本地产生与传播而来的TBSG，并识别可中断攻击路径的控制措施。案例验证表明，语法验证在信任边界处不充分，将信任边界假设显式化可补充Security-by-Design方法。

Conclusion: 语法验证在信任边界是必要但不充分的，系统化的TBSG分析框架（MDTBSG+TBSAM）通过显式化语义假设，能有效识别和缓解安全设计中的语义鸿沟问题。

Abstract: Modern systems use format-, protocol-, and signature-based mechanisms before accepting artifacts across trust boundaries. These mechanisms are necessary: they show that an artifact is well formed, protocol-compliant, or properly authenticated. They do not, however, show that the artifact satisfies the semantic security properties required by the receiving domain. A signed update or an authenticated token may therefore be accepted yet enable compromise. We call this condition a Trust Boundary Semantic Gap (TBSG): an artifact crosses a trust boundary and passes correctly implemented syntactic validation, but the assertions established by that pass are insufficient to satisfy the receiving domain's security requirements. TBSG concerns what remains unestablished after a syntactic pass, not absent checks or implementation bugs. Analyzing 75 publicly reported security incidents (2014-2025) at the boundary level, we organize semantic misalignment into a four-dimensional analysis model: Identity, Spatial, Temporal, and Interpretation (MDTBSG). Building on it, we develop Trust Boundary Semantic Analysis and Mitigation (TBSAM), a design-time framework that identifies TBSGs from design specifications, prioritizes them, traces propagated gaps to their originating boundary, and maps each to candidate architectural controls. We apply TBSAM to a retrospective reconstruction of the SolarWinds/SUNBURST supply-chain attack, showing how it makes receiving-domain assumptions explicit, separates locally originating from propagated gaps, and identifies controls that interrupt the path. These results suggest that syntactic validation, while necessary, is not sufficient at trust boundaries, and that making trust-boundary assumptions explicit can complement Security-by-Design.

</details>


### [32] [Resilient Liquid Democracy: Mitigating Voting Power Imbalances via Secure Delegation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01730)
*Zhuolun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全的流动民主机制，采用密封委托（基于去中心化定时释放加密）、排名多委托和个人备用选票，既能防止羊群效应和胁迫，又能应对委托方失败。在四个真实数据集上的评估表明：委托能否提升代表性准确度遵循"可恢复差距定律"；密封委托的主要优势在于结构性——显著降低权力集中度；排名多委托可大幅减少选票损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流动民主部署采用透明委托模式，导致选民易受从众效应影响、胁迫可被验证，且当高权重代表弃权时系统脆弱性显著。现有机制未能解决"专业意见路由"与"系统鲁棒性"之间的张力。

Method: 采用去中心化定时释放加密实现密封委托，在形成阶段加密隐藏委托选择以防止羊群效应和胁迫，最终计票阶段恢复公开可审计性；扩展协议加入排名多委托和个人备用选票以应对委托方失败；通过形式化证明确保预揭示保密性和重提交收据无关联性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集（市政参与式预算选举+校准调查、20个参与式预算选举、6万美国选民+客观能力指标）上评估发现：(1) 委托提升代表性准确度遵循"可恢复差距定律"——仅在弃权率高且系统性不具代表性时有效，否则中性或有害；(2) 密封委托的主要收益是结构性的——显著降低权力集中度而非直接提升准确性；(3) 排名多委托+个人备用选票在现实和针对性委托失败场景下大幅减少选票损失，结果在20个选举中可复现。

Conclusion: 密封委托机制通过加密隐藏委托选择，有效解决了透明流动民主的羊群效应和胁迫问题；委托的效用受条件限制（仅在特定场景有益）；排名多委托显著增强了系统鲁棒性。研究为流动民主的实际部署提供了重要的设计准则。

Abstract: Liquid democracy promises to improve collective decision-making by allowing voters to vote directly, delegate their voting power to trusted participants, or combine both approaches through fallback mechanisms. However, existing deployments typically rely on transparent delegation, which exposes voters to popularity-driven herding, makes coercion verifiable, and introduces systemic fragility when highly-backed delegates abstain. In this paper, we propose a secure liquid democracy mechanism that resolves the tension between informed expertise routing and systemic robustness. We introduce a sealed delegation regime using decentralized timed-release encryption, which cryptographically hides delegation choices during the formation phase to prevent herding and coercion, while restoring full public auditability for the final tally. To address delegate failures, we extend the protocol with ranked multi-delegation and personal fallback ballots. We formally prove pre-reveal secrecy and resubmission receipt-freeness for our protocol. Finally, we evaluate the mechanism on four real datasets, a municipal participatory-budgeting election with a calibration survey, twenty further participatory-budgeting elections, and 60,000 US voters with an objective competence measure. They show that whether delegation improves representational accuracy follows a recoverable-gap law; it helps only when abstention is large and systematically unrepresentative, and is otherwise neutral or harmful, with representative-style delegation safer than delegating to a competence elite. The benefit of sealed formation is primarily structural, sharply reducing power concentration rather than directly improving accuracy; and ranked multi-delegation with personal fallback ballots sharply reduces vote loss under realistic and targeted delegate failures, a result that replicates across all twenty elections.

</details>


### [33] [Knowledge Over Parameters: Evolving Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01742)
*Yuqiang Sun,Han Liu,Ying Li,Yiran Zhang,Zong Cao,Ziyun Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoVuln是一个自动化智能合约漏洞检测框架，通过将检测问题转化为程序化知识进化问题，仅需少量标记样本即可合成和优化检测逻辑。它采用IoC架构的解耦运行时和两阶段进化管线，在五种真实漏洞类型上达到71%宏平均F1分数，且进化后的知识可在不同模型间迁移，单次进化成本低于50美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能合约漏洞检测方法存在瓶颈：基于提示的方法依赖人工设计检测规则，而微调方法需要大量稀缺的标记数据集。智能合约漏洞主要是逻辑错误，其检测需要结构化的、逐步的程序化知识，但现有方法难以自动生成这类知识。

Method: 提出EvoVuln框架，将漏洞检测重构为程序化知识进化问题。核心机制包括：(1) 基于控制反转(IoC)架构的运行时，将检测规则编译为可执行策略，解耦确定性控制流与LLM语义推理；(2) 两阶段进化管线：冷启动阶段通过自动合成边界用例初始化和压力测试规则，少样本进化阶段仅用每个漏洞类型5个脆弱和5个安全样本将策略锚定于真实语义。整个过程无需参数更新，采用溯因语义调试来精炼规则。

Result: 在五种真实世界漏洞类型上，EvoVuln达到71%宏平均F1分数，优于所有基线方法。进化后的程序化知识可跨模型迁移：使轻量低成本模型超越大规模零样本模型19个百分点，且无需重新训练即可迁移到其他LLM，单次进化成本低于50美元。

Conclusion: EvoVuln证明了将漏洞检测转化为程序化知识进化问题的有效性，通过解耦架构和高效进化管线，以极低的标注成本和计算成本实现了优异的检测性能，并且知识具有良好的跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities are predominantly logic bugs whose detection requires structured, step-by-step procedural knowledge of attack patterns and contract semantics. Existing LLM-based methods struggle to generate this knowledge automatically: prompt-based methods rely on manually crafted detection rules, while fine-tuning requires massive labeled datasets that are inherently scarce in this domain. We present EvoVuln, an automated framework that reformulates vulnerability detection as a procedural knowledge evolution problem, synthesizing and refining detection logic using only a minimal number of labeled samples.
  To achieve this, EvoVuln introduces two key mechanisms. First, a Runtime with an Inversion of Control (IoC) architecture compiles detection rules into Executable Policies. This strictly decouples deterministic control flow from LLM semantic reasoning, ensuring faithful logical adherence and producing dense diagnostic telemetry for precise error localization. Second, a two-phase evolution pipeline refines the rule via abductive semantic debugging without any parameter updates: Cold Start bootstraps and stress-tests an initial rule using auto-synthesized corner cases; Few-Shot Evolving then grounds the policy in real-world semantics using only five vulnerable and five safe examples per vulnerability type.
  Evaluated across five real-world vulnerability types, EvoVuln achieves a 71% macro-average F1-score, outperforming all baselines. The evolved procedural knowledge is portable across models: it enables a lightweight, low-cost model to surpass a much larger zero-shot model by 19 percentage points, and transfers to other LLMs without retraining, at a one-time evolution cost under $50.

</details>

*Gabriel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需阈值的检查点审计方法，通过结合参考锚定的激活拒绝差距和权重恢复能量两个内部信号，在273个检查点注册表上实现了对拒绝机制被移除的开放权重模型的高效检测（AUROC 0.95），并分析了两种失效场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时防护措施只能对生成内容进行评分，无法在部署前检测开放权重检查点的拒绝机制是否被剥离，因此需要一种轻量级、无需训练且不依赖阈值的预部署审计方法。

Method: 结合两种廉价内部信号：参考锚定的激活拒绝差距（提供拒绝特异性）和基座到候选模型权重差异的权重恢复能量（提供召回率），两者负相关且标签互补，通过z-sum融合形成无阈值审计。

Result: 在涵盖Qwen、DeepSeek-distilled Qwen、Llama和Gemma的273个检查点注册表上，z-sum融合信号在57个公开消融模型与37个良性微调/合并/指令微调模型间达到AUROC 0.95，显著优于单一信号（0.84, 0.90）；Youden校准阈值在留出家族上达到平衡准确率0.89（FPR 0.11），仅漏检4/57个消融模型。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种有效的预部署分诊工具而非防篡改方案，其有效性依赖于经过认证的参考锚点，且结论受限于评估所用的注册表范围；同时识别了两种失效场景（零训练欺骗和白盒攻击绕过阈值）。

Abstract: Can a platform tell, before deployment, whether an open-weight checkpoint has had its refusal mechanism stripped? Runtime guards cannot: they score generations, not the artifact. We combine two cheap internal signals, a reference-anchored activation refusal-gap and a weight-recovery energy of the base-to-candidate weight difference, into a threshold-free checkpoint audit. The two are negatively correlated and label-complementary: the gap supplies refusal-specificity and the weight energy supplies recall. On a 273-checkpoint registry spanning Qwen, DeepSeek-distilled Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, their z-sum separates 57 public abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes, merges, and instruction-tunes at AUROC 0.95, significantly above either signal alone (0.84, 0.90), and a Youden-calibrated threshold transfers to held-out families at balanced accuracy 0.89 (FPR 0.11), missing only 4 of 57. We then map two failures, in order of severity: a spoofed reference evades both axes with no training (ΔW=0, \r{ho}=1 by construction), and a white-box owner trains a checkpoint past the threshold while it stays guard-unsafe and coherent. The audit is effective triage, not tamper-proofing: it presumes an attested reference, and its claims are bounded by the registry we evaluate it on.

</details>
