For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [148] [Multi-Rate Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Wall-Supported Bipedal Locomotion of Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01574)
*Taizoon Chunawala,Jeeseop Kim,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多速率非线性模型预测控制（MR-NMPC）的分层规划与控制框架，使四足机器人在受限环境中利用墙壁辅助实现混合双足运动，通过高层MR-NMPC联合优化接触点与质心/姿态轨迹，结合低层全身控制器（WBC）跟踪参考，在崎岖地形和外部扰动下表现出鲁棒性，成功率比传统启发式落脚策略高出2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时轨迹优化面临显著挑战：控制器需同时规划接触点和连续轨迹（质心与姿态），并兼顾非线性动力学、单边接触约束、欠驱动以及动力学切换特性。

Method: 高层采用MR-NMPC，基于单刚体动力学模型动态规划离散接触点轨迹和连续质心/姿态轨迹；低层采用基于虚拟约束和二次规划的全身控制器（WBC），强制执行全阶动力学并跟踪MR-NMPC参考。

Result: 在Unitree A1四足机器人上的数值仿真验证了该方法在崎岖地形和外部扰动下的鲁棒性；与启发式落脚策略的常规MPC相比，MR-NMPC在高速度不规则地形上的成功率高出2.9倍。

Conclusion: 将接触点规划纳入多速率最优控制框架能够增强动态稳定性，所提出的分层框架有效解决了受限环境下四足机器人墙壁辅助双足运动的实时优化问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel layered planning and control framework based on multi-rate nonlinear model predictive control (MR-NMPC) that enables quadrupedal robots to perform hybrid bipedal locomotion with wall-assisted support in constrained environments. Real-time trajectory optimization for this locomotion presents significant challenges, as the controller must simultaneously plan for both the contact points and the continuous trajectories of the robot's center of mass (CoM) and orientation within the robot's nonlinear dynamics while accounting for unilateral contact constraints, underactuation, and the switching nature of the robot's dynamics. At the high level of the control framework, an MR-NMPC is proposed, which dynamically plans both the discrete-time trajectories of the contact points and the continuous-time trajectories of the CoM and orientation, using a single rigid body (SRB) dynamics model. By incorporating contact-point planning within the multi-rate optimal control framework, this approach enhances dynamic stability compared to heuristic foot placement strategies. At the low level of the control framework, a nonlinear whole-body controller (WBC) based on virtual constraints and a quadratic program enforces full-order dynamics and tracks the MR-NMPC references. The proposed approach is validated through extensive numerical simulations demonstrating the robust wall-assisted bipedal locomotion of a Unitree A1 quadrupedal robot on rough terrains and under external disturbances in a constrained environment. Comparative analysis shows that the proposed MR-NMPC achieves a 2.9 times higher success rate compared to conventional MPC with heuristic-based foot placement strategies in negotiating irregular terrain at high speeds.

</details>


### [149] [Path planning for unmanned naval surface vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01631)
*Daniel G. Schwartz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合全局与局部路径规划器的新方法，用于无人水面艇（USV）同时避开固定和移动障碍物。全局规划器融合了Grassfire、改进版Grassfire和新型概率路线图三种算法；局部规划器则基于障碍物相对于全局路径的运动方向进行高层决策，引导USV从障碍物后方绕行。仿真实验（含D*算法对比）验证了该方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有USV避障方法多针对固定障碍物，移动障碍物（如船只、游泳者等）的实时规避仍具挑战性，且缺乏能同时有效处理两类障碍物并引导USV从障碍物后方绕行的系统化方案。

Method: 全局路径规划器：组合三种算法——经典Grassfire、改进Grassfire以及新型概率路线图（PRM变体）。局部路径规划器：基于观测到的障碍物相对USV全局路径的运动方向，采用高层决策逻辑，系统性地引导USV从障碍物后方绕行，而非平行等待通行时机。

Result: 仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并与经典D*算法及其他动态路径规划系统进行了比较，表明该方法能引导USV从移动障碍物后方绕行，而D*等算法不具备此特性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合规划框架能够有效解决USV同时规避固定与移动障碍物的问题，其中局部规划器的后方绕行策略相比传统方法提供了更优的规避路径。

Abstract: There nowadays is a myriad of approaches to real-time avoidance of fixed obstacles for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) and, to a lesser extent, also the task of avoiding moving obstacles such as boats, ships, swimmers, and other USVs, but both topics still present challenges. This paper offers novel approaches to both of these problems. It uses a combination of a global path planner, which finds a path from a start point to a goal point that avoids fixed obstacles (given that their locations are known in advance), and a local path planner, which can circumnavigate a moving obstacle (as well as any previously unknown fixed obstacles). The global planner is novel in that it employs a combination of three path planners, one known in the literature as Grassfire, one that is a new modification of Grassfire, and one that is a new, and arguably more intuitive, version of the well-known Probabilistic Roadmap. The local planner is novel in that it employs a higher-level decision logic based on its observations regarding the direction of movement of the obstacle relative to the USVs global path. This logic enables the USV to determine the best strategy for avoiding the obstacle by systematically routing the vehicle behind the obstacle rather than running parallel to it until the opportunity to pass appears. Simulations are provided that validate these claims. For comparison with other systems, the simulations include an implementation of the well-known D* algorithm, and the discussion covers additional dynamic path planning systems, which, like D*, do not necessarily route the vehicle behind the moving obstacle.

</details>


### [150] [One Demonstration Is Enough for Real-World Robotic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01651)
*Yuwan Liu,Hongze Yu,Song Liu,Yuhan Wang,Junge Zhang,Yaodong Yang,Yuanpei Chen,Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoSERL提出一个基于单次演示的框架，通过滑动窗口干预、安全恢复和自动终止机制，在现实世界机器人强化学习中实现自动化干预，在六个接触密集型操作任务中优于需要20次演示的SERL等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理硬件上学习机器人控制策略面临数据收集成本高和奖励函数设计困难两大挑战。现有方法要么需要大量演示数据，要么在训练过程中依赖持续的人工干预。

Method: 提出AutoSERL框架，包含三种互补机制：1) 滑动窗口干预机制，持续引导探索防止局部最优和不安全偏差；2) 安全恢复机制，通过预定轨迹恢复点检测和纠正失败状态；3) 干预终止准则，在策略能独立完成任务时自动禁用引导，保留其探索优势。

Result: 在两个机器人平台的六个接触密集型操作任务（插入、悬挂、铰链类任务）上，AutoSERL一致优于SERL（20次演示）、行为克隆和MILES（单次模仿学习基线），匹配HIL-SERL的性能，插入任务达到100%成功率，且对位置变化具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 仅需单次演示即可实现现实世界机器人强化学习的全自动化干预，显著降低了数据需求和人工干预成本。

Abstract: Learning effective robot control policies on physical hardware is challenging due to costly data collection and the difficulty of reward specification. Prior work has incorporated demonstrations into reinforcement learning (RL), yet existing approaches either require large numbers of demonstrations or depend on continuous human intervention during training. To address these limitations, we present AutoSERL, a framework that leverages a single demonstration to fully automate the intervention process in real-world robot RL. The framework includes three complementary mechanisms to accomplish certain tasks: a sliding window intervention mechanism that continuously guides exploration to prevent local optima and unsafe deviations, a safety recovery mechanism that detects and corrects failure states via predefined trajectory recovery points, and an intervention termination criterion that automatically disables guidance once the policy can independently complete the task, preserving its exploration advantage. We evaluate AutoSERL on six contact-intensive manipulation tasks across two robot platforms, spanning insertion, hanging, and hinge-based tasks. AutoSERL consistently outperforms SERL initialized with 20 demonstrations, behavior cloning, and MILES -- a dedicated one-shot imitation learning baseline -- across all tasks while matching HIL-SERL, achieves 100% success rate on insertion tasks, and demonstrates improved robustness to positional variations, all from a single demonstration. Code and videos are available on our project website: https://autoserl.github.io/.

</details>


### [151] [Imagining the Sense of Touch: Touch-Informed Manipulation via Imagined Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01684)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Jyun-Chi Hu,Yosuke Saka,Quan Khanh Luu,Jiuzhou Lei,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Bihao Zhang,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacImag框架，无需触觉传感器，仅通过视觉和本体感觉预测触觉信号并指导操作策略。在6个模拟和4个真实任务中验证，想象触觉场提升接触敏感任务44.4%，想象触觉图像提升纹理敏感任务23.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感在灵巧操作中潜力巨大，但实际部署受限于硬件脆弱、校准繁琐和维护成本高。核心问题：机器人能否在部署时不使用触觉传感器，却仍能受益于触觉知识？

Method: TacImag框架：利用配对视觉-触觉演示数据训练预测模型，从视觉和本体感觉推断触觉观测（力场或触觉图像）；测试时仅需视觉输入，通过生成的想象触觉信号引导操作策略。

Result: 6个模拟+4个真实任务实验：想象力场使接触敏感任务平均提升44.4%；想象触觉图像使纹理敏感任务平均提升23.3%。触觉想象的效果取决于触觉表征与任务需求之间的匹配程度。

Conclusion: 触觉想象不仅是对缺失触觉测量值的简单恢复，更是一种接触感知监督形式——将细微的视觉交互线索转化为操作策略更易利用的表征。

Abstract: Tactile sensing can substantially improve contact-rich robotic manipulation, yet its practical deployment remains limited by the fragility, calibration requirements, and maintenance burden of tactile hardware. This raises a fundamental question: can robots benefit from tactile knowledge without requiring tactile sensors at deployment? We present TacImag, a tactile imagination framework that predicts tactile observations from vision and proprioception and uses the generated signals to guide manipulation policies. Trained from paired visuotactile demonstrations, TacImag enables touch-informed manipulation using only visual observations at test time. We evaluate TacImag on six simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks. Across simulation and real-world experiments, imagined tactile observations consistently improve manipulation performance without requiring tactile hardware. In real-world experiments, imagined force fields improve contact-sensitive tasks by 44.4% on average, whereas imagined tactile images improve texture-sensitive tasks by 23.3%, revealing that the effectiveness of tactile imagination depends strongly on the relationship between tactile representation and task requirements. Our results further suggest that tactile imagination does not simply recover missing tactile measurements. Instead, it acts as a form of contact-aware supervision that transforms subtle visual interaction cues into representations that are easier for manipulation policies to exploit.

</details>


### [152] [CoRe: Combined Rewards with Vision-Language Model Feedback for Preference-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01721)
*Hexian Ni,Tao Lu,Yinghao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRe框架将奖励分解为形式奖励（FR）和残差奖励（RR），利用视觉语言模型（VLM）自动设计与优化奖励，在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操控任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计奖励困难且可能导致次优策略，而从偏好学习奖励效率低且训练不稳定，因此需要一种无需人工参与、高效且与偏好对齐的自动奖励构建方法。

Method: 提出CoRe混合框架：形式奖励模块（FRM）利用VLM迭代设计和优化FR；残差奖励模块（RRM）从视频级偏好中学习RR，两者协同实现偏好对齐的奖励自动构建。

Result: 在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操控任务上，CoRe在策略学习有效性和效率方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将奖励分解为FR和RR并利用VLM自动构建，能够高效实现偏好对齐的策略学习，无需人工参与。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Hand-crafted rewards are often difficult to specify and may lead to suboptimal policies, while learned rewards from preferences can suffer from inefficiency and unstable training. Inspired by the dual nature of human learning explored in cognitive science, we decompose rewards into two complementary components: Formal Rewards (FR), explicitly designed based on task knowledge, and Residual Rewards (RR), learned from observations to capture implicit and nuanced preferences. Based on this decomposition, we propose CoRe, a hybrid framework that integrates FR and RR with vision-language models (VLMs) feedback to achieve preference-aligned policies without human involvement. Our contributions are twofold: (1) We propose a Formal Reward Module (FRM) that leverages VLMs to iteratively design and optimize FR based on task knowledge and preference feedback, enabling the continual improvement of policy during training; (2) We introduce a Residual Reward Module (RRM) that learns RR from video-level preference by employing VLMs to generate preference labels and capturing nuanced rewards that complement FR, ensuring alignment with human intent. Through the synergy of FRM and RRM, CoRe enables the automatic construction of reliable rewards that are efficient and preference-aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoRe outperforms existing approaches in terms of policy learning effectiveness and efficiency on ten robotic manipulation tasks in simulation and five real-worlds. Videos can be found on our project website: https://core-2026.github.io/

</details>


### [153] [Lightweight Safe Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01794)
*Shenghui Zhang,YuXuan Gao,Songwei Zhao,Jifeng Hu,Zijing Zhang,Hechang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种安全约束下的感知-控制一体化无人机导航框架，采用轻量级网络编码稀疏观测、基于拉格朗日的安全PPO算法和课程学习，在密集障碍环境和高速飞行下实现了更高的成功率、安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法缺乏显式安全机制，导致不安全探索和不稳定训练；安全RL方法通过将策略输出投影到安全动作集上可能引入不稳定性；且多数学习方法依赖密集输入或大网络，计算负担重，难以轻量化部署。

Method: 1) 轻量级网络：采用非对称和深度可分离卷积，将稀疏观测编码为碰撞风险感知特征；2) 将任务建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），采用分层控制架构；3) 基于拉格朗日的安全PPO算法求解；4) 课程学习提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在不同障碍密度和飞行速度的实验下，相比现有强化学习基线方法，实现了更高的成功率、更好的安全性和更高的效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全约束感知-控制一体化框架有效解决了无人机在密集环境中自主导航的安全性、稳定性和轻量化部署问题。

Abstract: With the rapid development of autonomous aerial systems, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed in applications such as inspection, environmental monitoring, and rescue, creating growing demand for reliable autonomous navigation. However, autonomous UAV navigation in dense environments remains challenging under sparse perception and dynamic constraints. Most reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack explicit safety mechanisms, leading to unsafe exploration, unstable training, and risky behaviors, especially during high-speed flight. Even in safe RL approaches, safety is often enforced by projecting policy outputs onto a safe action set, which may introduce instability. Meanwhile, many learning-based methods rely on dense inputs or large networks, increasing computational burden and limiting lightweight onboard deployment. Facing the above challenges, we propose a safety-constrained perception-control integrated framework for UAV navigation. A lightweight network encodes sparse observations into collision-risk-aware features using asymmetric and depthwise separable convolutions. We formulate the task as a constrained Markov decision process within a hierarchical control architecture and solve it using a Lagrangian-based safe PPO algorithm. Curriculum learning further improves training stability. Experiments with varying obstacle densities and flight speeds demonstrate higher success rates, improved safety, and better efficiency than existing reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [154] [VLA-Corrector: Lightweight Detect-and-Correct Inference for Adaptive Action Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01804)
*Yi Pan,Miao Pan,Qi Lu,Jiaming Huang,Man Zhang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Jie Zhang,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Corrector提出了一种轻量级修正推理框架，通过潜在空间视觉监控器（LVM）在线检测视觉动态偏差并触发截断与在线梯度引导（OGG）重规划，在不修改VLA骨干权重的前提下，将固定动作块策略转变为事件触发自适应动作视界，减轻了静态视界下执行鲁棒性与调用频率之间的权衡，显著提升了长时域、接触丰富的机器人操作任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基础模型采用固定动作块（action chunk）机制以降低策略调用频率，但"先预测再盲目执行"的范式在接触丰富的物理交互中缺乏闭环反应能力：局部微小扰动会在开环盲区内快速放大，导致累积误差和任务失败。

Method: 提出VLA-Corrector框架，包含两个关键组件：1) 潜在空间视觉监控器（LVM），持续比较预测视觉特征与实际视觉特征的演化，在线检测视觉动态偏差；2) 在线梯度引导（OGG），在检测到持续偏差时触发截断事件，丢弃剩余过时动作并执行修正重规划。该机制自然形成事件触发的自适应动作视界。

Result: 无需修改VLA骨干权重或重新训练，可集成到不同VLA模型中；在保持动作块效率优势的同时，显著中断累积错误，提升了长时域、接触丰富的机器人操作任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLA-Corrector通过检测-修正机制有效缓解了静态动作视界下执行鲁棒性与策略调用频率之间的权衡，为动作块化的VLA策略提供了轻量级、即插即用的闭环校正方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently achieved strong progress in embodied intelligence. To reduce policy-call frequency while preserving temporal coherence, most generative policies adopt an action chunk mechanism, executing multiple future actions in an open-loop manner under a fixed action horizon. However, this "predict-then-blindly-execute" paradigm sacrifices closed-loop reactivity: in contact-rich physical interactions, even small local perturbations can rapidly amplify within the open-loop blind spot, leading to compounding errors and ultimately task failure. To address this limitation, we propose VLA-Corrector, a lightweight corrective inference framework for action-chunked VLA policies. Without modifying the backbone policy weights, VLA-Corrector introduces a lightweight Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM) that continuously compares predicted and actual visual feature evolution, enabling online detection of visual dynamics deviations. Once persistent deviation is detected, the system triggers a truncation event, discards the remaining stale actions, and invokes corrective replanning via Online Gradient Guidance (OGG). The detect-and-correct mechanism of VLA-Corrector naturally induces an event-triggered adaptive action horizon: it preserves long-horizon execution when the current chunk remains reliable, and invokes short-horizon corrective replanning when execution begins to drift. In doing so, VLA-Corrector mitigates the trade-off imposed by static horizons between execution robustness and policy-call frequency. It can be integrated into different VLA models without further retraining the VLA backbone, interrupting compounding errors while preserving much of the efficiency benefit of action chunking and substantially improving robustness in long-horizon, contact-rich robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [155] [DL-SLAM: Enabling High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting SLAM in Dynamic Environments based on Dual-Level Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01860)
*Ziheng Xu,Qingfeng Li,Xuefeng Liu,Chen Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DL-SLAM 提出双层次概率框架，通过语义与几何信息融合生成动态概率图，在像素级和实例级进行动态物体判别与修剪，实现了无伪影静态地图构建，跟踪精度提升最高达13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态SLAM方法要么直接丢弃预定义动态物体，忽略瞬态静止物体对位姿估计的几何约束价值；要么虽能利用瞬态静止物体，却错误地将其融入静态地图导致伪影，且纯几何信息导致不确定性图的物体边界模糊。

Method: 提出双层次概率框架：(1) 融合语义与几何信息计算像素级动态概率图；(2) 将像素级概率提升到3D并聚合得到实例级物体动态概率；(3) 基于物体级概率对动态高斯进行类别性修剪，生成无伪影静态地图；(4) 静态地图反向为像素级概率提供几何一致性的引导优化。

Result: DL-SLAM 在跟踪精度上提升最高达13%，并能生成高保真语义地图。

Conclusion: 双层次概率框架有效解决了动态物体误融入静态地图的问题，通过语义与几何融合提升了不确定性图的边界清晰度，实现更优的位姿估计与地图质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in dense dynamic Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). Prevailing methods typically discard predefined dynamic objects, ignoring that transiently static objects offer valuable geometric constraints for pose estimation. A recent work attempts to leverage this potential by employing per-pixel uncertainty maps to quantify the magnitude of motion. While this approach enables transiently static objects to enhance pose estimation, it erroneously integrates these objects into the static map, resulting in persistent artifacts. Moreover, its reliance on purely geometric information leads to ambiguous object boundaries in the uncertainty maps. To overcome these limitations, we present DL-SLAM, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system built upon a novel dual-level probabilistic framework. Our method computes dynamic probability maps by combining semantic and geometric information. These pixel-level probabilities are lifted to 3D and aggregated to derive an object-level dynamic probability for each instance. Object-level probability enables the categorical pruning of dynamic Gaussians, resulting in an artifact-free static map. The static map, in turn, provides a geometrically consistent guidance to refine the pixel-wise probabilities, enhancing their reliability. Experimental results demonstrate that DL-SLAM outperforms existing approaches, improving tracking accuracy by up to 13\% while generating high-fidelity semantic maps.

</details>


### [156] [SPLC: Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01925)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Fu,Haiwen Hu,Shiquan Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLC算法，通过社会偏好反馈机制自动生成偏好数据，消除对精细奖励函数设计的依赖，在离线强化学习框架下实现人群机器人的社会合规导航，在仿真和真实实验中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在人群机器人导航中潜力巨大，但行人运动固有的复杂性使得设计有效的奖励函数来促进社会合规的机器人行为一直是一个持续的挑战。

Method: 提出Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC)算法，核心创新是引入社会偏好反馈机制，通过原则性的偏好评估标准自动生成偏好数据，显式考虑行人动力学复杂性，减轻奖励偏差并系统量化广泛的社会规范。

Result: 将SPLC与离线RL方法结合的广泛实验在标准性能指标上一致优于最先进的基线；TurtleBot4上的真实世界实验进一步验证了SPLC在现实人机共存场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPLC通过社会偏好学习有效消除了对精细奖励函数设计的需求，提升了离线强化学习在人群机器人导航中的社会合规性，仿真和真实实验均证明了其优越性能。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant potential for crowd robot navigation in human-robot coexistence applications. However, the inherent complexity of pedestrian motion renders the design of effective reward functions for promoting socially compliant robot behaviors a persistent challenge. This paper proposes a Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC) algorithm to eliminate the need for detailed reward design. Its core innovation lies in the introduction of a social preference feedback mechanism to automatically generate preference data through principled preference evaluation criteria. By explicitly accounting for the intricacies of pedestrian dynamics, the pipeline mitigates the reward bias and facilitates the systematic quantification of broad social norms, thereby fostering socially compliant behaviors. Extensive experiments integrating SPLC with offline RL methods demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across standard performance metrics. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the TurtleBot4 further validate the effectiveness of SPLC in practical human-robot coexistence settings. Our code and video demos are available at https://github.com/sklus949/SPLC.

</details>


### [157] [PhysMani: Physics-principled 3D World Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01938)
*Peng Yun,Shouwang Huang,Hao Li,Jinxi Li,Jianan Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysMani提出了一种结合物理原则的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知动作策略模型的框架，用于解决非结构化3D环境中快速动态目标的操控问题，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均取得优异成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型在处理快速动态目标时，缺乏准确的3D几何建模和有物理意义的未来预测能力。

Method: 构建物理原则的3D高斯世界模型，通过在线优化学习无散度高斯速度场实现物理一致的未来动态预测；策略模型通过可学习token的交叉注意力模块整合预测的3D场景未来动态信息。

Result: 在包含16个任务的PhysMani-Bench动态操控基准上，PhysMani在仿真和真实机器人实验中均展现出优于强基线的成功率。

Conclusion: 耦合物理原则世界模型与未来感知策略模型能有效提升快速动态目标操控的性能，验证了物理一致性建模在具身AI中的重要性。

Abstract: Manipulating fast and dynamically moving targets in unstructured 3D environments remains challenging for embodied AI. Existing visual-language-action models and world models struggle with accurate 3D geometry and physically meaningful forecasting. We propose PhysMani, a framework that couples a physics-principled 3D Gaussian world model with a future-aware action policy model. The world model learns a divergence-free Gaussian velocity field via online optimization for fast and physically grounded future dynamics prediction. The policy model integrates the predicted 3D scene future dynamics through a learnable token based cross-attention module. We introduce PhysMani-Bench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark with 16 tasks, and demonstrate a superior success rate over strong baselines in both simulation and real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [158] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Way of Water框架，通过浏览器端的时序合成编舞工具（Way of Water Studio），将自主水面船队作为分布式编舞平台，利用层流喷嘴和多区域照明实现从2D水面到3D体积域的扩展，并在苏黎世湖（18艘船）和威尼斯双年展（8艘船）完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放水域的机器人编舞受非线性流体动力学约束，环境干扰和非线性系统动态带来巨大挑战，现有方法局限于水面像素级表达，缺乏面向非编程艺术家的高性能水上机器人编排工具。

Method: 提出Way of Water框架，包括：(1) Way of Water Studio浏览器端时序合成编舞工具，将船队视为类似DAW的乐器；(2) 顺次凸规划（SCP）用于轨迹生成，模型预测控制（MPC）用于扰动抑制；(3) 完整的物理-信息层：定制全向底盘、状态估计与控制栈、LTE/MQTT通信链路及RTK-GPS时间同步；(4) 层流喷嘴与多区域照明实现3D体积域表达。

Result: 在苏黎世湖完成18艘船的天鹅湖演绎，在威尼斯Forte Marghera完成8艘船的时空存在2025演示，验证了系统的可行性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为流体机器人集群的设计与部署建立了基础参考，通过将高性能水下/水面机器人控制抽象为可视化时间线工具，降低了非编程艺术家使用门槛。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [159] [A Stereo Visual SLAM System Using Object-Level Motion Estimation and Geometric Filtering Based on Cross Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02005)
*Sujan Kumar Dhali,Bhaskar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCD SLAM是一个动态双目视觉SLAM框架，通过联合处理场景中的动态物体和动态特征，在ORB-SLAM2基础上引入"交叉视差"几何方法检测动态特征点，并结合3D目标检测与卡尔曼滤波进行物体级运动分类，显著提升了动态环境下的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉SLAM系统在动态环境中因静态世界假设而失效，现有方法难以同时有效地处理动态物体和动态特征点带来的干扰。

Method: 提出交叉视差（cross disparity）几何方法，利用时间和立体不一致性检测动态特征点；集成SMOKE 3D目标检测模块与卡尔曼滤波进行物体级运动分类，实现动静场景元素的鲁棒分离。

Result: 在KITTI Odometry和KITTI Raw数据集上相比ORB-SLAM2及多种SOTA动态SLAM方法，轨迹精度显著提升；消融实验验证了交叉视差模块的有效性，能检测到3D目标检测遗漏的动态特征。

Conclusion: OCD SLAM通过特征级与物体级联合的运动分析方法，有效解决了动态环境下的SLAM问题，交叉视差模块对检测3D检测方案遗漏的动态特征具有互补作用。

Abstract: This paper presents OCD SLAM, a dynamic stereo visual SLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM2 by jointly addressing dynamic objects and dynamic features in the scene. Usual visual SLAM systems operating in dynamic environments often fail in the presence of moving objects, due to the static-world assumption used in pose estimation and mapping. To address this predicament, we introduce a novel geometric approach based on the discrepancy between disparity and a newly proposed notion called ``cross disparity'', which exploits both temporal and stereo inconsistency to identify dynamic feature points. Complementary to this feature-level motion analysis, OCD SLAM integrates a 3D object detection module (SMOKE) with Kalman filter-based object tracking to perform object-level motion classification, enabling robust separation of static and dynamic scene elements for accurate pose estimation. The proposed approach has been evaluated on various sequences from the KITTI Odometry and KITTI Raw datasets. Results demonstrate that OCD SLAM achieves significant improvement in trajectory accuracy compared to ORB-SLAM2 and several state-of-the-art dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the cross disparity module in the KITTI Raw dataset and show that this method is able to detect dynamic features that are missed by the 3D object detection scheme alone.

</details>


### [160] [Cross-Platform Control for Autonomous Surface Vehicles via Adaptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02037)
*Ruiheng Jiang,Thomas Bi,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应强化学习方法，通过教师-学生架构学习平台动力学的潜在表征，实现单一策略在未知动力学水面无人艇上的零样本跨平台部署，在真实实验中比非自适应基线提升最高58%的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器大多针对单一平台设计，无法直接迁移到不同 hydrodynamic 和 actuation 特性的水面无人艇上，需要一种能够零样本跨平台部署的通用轨迹跟踪策略。

Method: 采用部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程框架，通过教师-学生架构学习平台动力学的潜在表征，将交互历史作为策略条件；在仿真中随机化艇动力学参数进行训练，无需高保真水动力仿真器，直接零样本部署到真实平台。

Result: 在两个不同真实水面无人艇平台上，自适应策略的位置平均绝对误差比非自适应学习基线低最多58%，接近平台专用调优控制器的跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 基于简单解析动力学模型训练的强化学习策略，通过潜在动力学表征学习，能够有效实现跨平台零样本迁移，在真实无人艇上取得接近专用控制器的性能。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles vary widely in hydrodynamic and actuation characteristics, yet most controllers are designed for single-platform deployment. We present an adaptive reinforcement learning approach for trajectory tracking that enables zero-shot cross-platform deployment using a single policy. Since the deployment platform's dynamics are unknown to the policy, we address cross-platform generalization with the standard partial-observability approach of conditioning on interaction history, employing a teacher-student architecture in which a learned module infers a latent representation of the platform dynamics. The policy is trained in simulation under randomized vessel dynamics and is deployed zero-shot to two real-world platforms without any fine-tuning, despite relying on a simple analytical dynamics model rather than a high-fidelity hydrodynamic simulator. In real-world experiments on two different platforms, the adaptive policy outperforms non-adaptive learning-based baselines by up to 58% in position mean absolute error while approaching the tracking accuracy of a platform-specific tuned controller.

</details>


### [161] [Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092)
*Liuhaichen Yang,Zhuang Jiang,Chenchao Sheng,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Guided Action Flow框架，在预训练的流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略（SmolVLA）基础上，通过冻结基础策略并使用学习到的动作块评判器（critic）在推理时引导逆向流采样，无需重新训练策略，在LIBERO操作任务上取得了一定性能提升，但评判器泛化性和不确定性感知引导仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用流匹配VLA策略在推理时通过迭代传输过程生成动作块的特点，探索无需重训基础策略即可在测试时进行引导的机会，以提升策略性能。

Method: 保持预训练SmolVLA策略冻结，训练一个动作块评判器（critic），该评判器从真实成功与失败rollout中学习，可条件于冻结SmolVLA语言路径中的任务描述特征，在采样过程中仅通过动作梯度引导逆向流采样。

Result: 单任务评判器在LIBERO任务的一个种子窗口将成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%，另一窗口从82.0%提升至86.0%；多族任务描述评判器将验证成功率从46.0%提升至56.0%，锁定保留测试集从65.0%提升至67.5%。

Conclusion: Q引导推理在冻结流匹配VLA策略上可行，但评判器泛化性和不确定性感知引导仍是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action policies generate robot action chunks through an iterative transport process, creating an opportunity for test-time guidance without retraining the base policy. We study this opportunity in Guided Action Flow, an inference-time framework that keeps a pretrained SmolVLA policy frozen and uses a learned action-chunk critic to guide its reverse-time flow sampler. The critic is trained from real success and failure rollouts, can condition on task-description features from the frozen SmolVLA language pathway, and is used only through action gradients during sampling. We evaluate the approach on LIBERO manipulation tasks. A single-task critic improves success from 68.0% to 82.0% on one seed window and from 82.0% to 86.0% on another. A multi-family task-description critic improves validation success from 46.0% to 56.0%, while the locked held-out test gain is positive but modest, from 65.0% to 67.5%. These results support the feasibility of Q-guided inference for frozen flow-matching VLA policies, while showing that critic generalization and uncertainty-aware guidance remain the central bottlenecks.

</details>


### [162] [Bridge-WA: Predicting Where and How the World Changes for Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02195)
*Yongjie Bai,Hanting Wang,Mingtong Dai,Qijun Zhong,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge-WA提出一种轻量化世界-动作框架，通过从冻结的未来变化教师模型中蒸馏出三个紧凑先验（未来令牌、变化图、运动流图），在不依赖密集未来图像生成的情况下提升机器人操控的泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型依赖大规模生成式世界模型或密集未来 rollout，计算成本高且将容量浪费在与控制耦合弱的视觉细节上，亟需一种轻量级方案聚焦动作相关的场景变化。

Method: 从冻结的未来变化教师模型蒸馏出三个紧凑先验：未来令牌（预期结果）、变化图（干预支持）、运动流图（局部过渡方向）。通过WorldBridge模块利用多源注意力记忆和时空偏置将先验条件化到动作变换器上，推理时移除教师模型。

Result: 在VLABench、RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-Plus及真实机器人评估中，Bridge-WA在任务成功率、进度和鲁棒性上均有提升，尤其在分布外视觉偏移下增益显著。

Conclusion: 聚焦动作生成在场景将如何变化及何处变化上，抑制背景、光照、干扰物等无关外观因素，实现更好的泛化且无需部署时密集未来图像生成。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language-action models benefit from large vision-language priors, but effective manipulation also requires anticipating action-relevant scene changes. Existing world-action models often rely on large generative world models or dense future rollouts, which are expensive and spend capacity on visual details weakly coupled to control. We present Bridge-WA, a lightweight world-action framework that distills a frozen future-change teacher into three compact priors: future tokens for intended outcomes, change maps for intervention support, and motion-flow maps for local transition direction. A WorldBridge conditions the action transformer on these priors through multi-source attention memories and spatial-temporal biases, while the teacher model is removed at inference. Across VLABench, RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-Plus and real-robot evaluations, Bridge-WA improves task success, progress, and robustness, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution visual shifts. By focusing action generation on where and how the scene will change, Bridge-WA suppresses nuisance appearance factors such as background, lighting, and distractors, leading to better generalization without deployment-time dense future-image generation. Code and visualizations are available at: https://hcplab-sysu.github.io/BRIDGE-WA .

</details>


### [163] [Actuator Reality Shaping for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02205)
*Satoshi Yamamori,Koji Ishihara,Kentaro Minamikawa,Kiyoharu Ohomori,Taiyo Yazaki,Norikazu Sugimoto,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“执行器现实塑造”（Actuator Reality Shaping）的 sim-to-real 迁移新范式。该方法不修改仿真器去匹配真实硬件，而是通过设计一个二自由度前馈-反馈控制器，将真实执行器的闭环行为塑造成仿真中使用的理想二阶参考动力学。这使得在仿真中仅基于该参考模型训练的策略能够零样本地直接部署到真实机器人上，无需微调或学习执行器模型。在单关节伺服、7-DOF 机械臂、轮腿机器人和人形机器人上的实验验证了该方法显著降低了 sim-to-real 跟踪误差并提升了任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的 sim-to-real 迁移通常受限于仿真中理想执行器动力学与真实物理电机非线性、硬件依赖行为之间的差异。传统方法（系统辨识、域随机化、学习执行器模型）试图提高仿真器保真度来弥合这一差距，但本文作者提出了另一种思路：不修改仿真器，而是改变真实执行器的行为以匹配仿真。

Method: 为每个关节配备一个二自由度（2-DoF）前馈-反馈控制器，将参考-响应整形与鲁棒镇定解耦，从而使真实执行器的闭环行为匹配仿真中使用的理想二阶参考动力学。策略训练仅使用该参考模型，无需任务级微调或学习执行器模型。

Result: 在单关节高减速比伺服（带外部负载）、7-DOF 机械臂到达任务上，与标准伺服控制和代表性的 real-to-sim-to-real 基线相比，该方法显著降低了 sim-to-real 跟踪误差并提升了零样本任务性能。进一步在轮腿机器人和人形机器人上验证了零样本迁移的成功。

Conclusion: 执行器现实塑造能够作为一种可复用的标准化接口，适用于多种机器人平台，为 sim-to-real 迁移提供了一种新的有效范式。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer in robot learning is often limited by discrepancies between the ideal actuator dynamics assumed during policy training and the nonlinear, hardware-dependent behavior of physical motors. While conventional approaches attempt to bridge this gap by increasing simulator fidelity through system identification, domain randomization, or learned actuator models, we introduce an alternative paradigm: actuator reality shaping. Instead of modifying the simulator to match the real world, our method shapes the closed-loop behavior of physical actuators to match the idealized second-order reference dynamics used in simulation. By equipping each joint with a two-degree-of-freedom feedforward--feedback controller, we decouple reference-response shaping from robust stabilization, thereby providing a standardized actuator interface for reinforcement learning policies. As a result, policies trained only with the prescribed reference model can be deployed zero-shot on real hardware without task-level fine-tuning or learned actuator models. We validate the approach on a single-joint high-gear-ratio servo under external loads and a 7-DOF robotic arm reaching task, where actuator reality shaping substantially reduces sim-to-real tracking error and improves zero-shot task performance compared with standard servo-control and representative real-to-sim-to-real baselines. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer on a wheeled-legged robot driving over a slope and a humanoid robot walking, suggesting that actuator reality shaping can serve as a reusable interface for robot learning across diverse hardware platforms.

</details>


### [164] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Wentao Zhang,Masaki Kitagawa,Zicen Xiong,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFL-S，一种基于语言条件流场预测的低级视觉-语言-动作框架，用于连续轨迹生成，在连续时间Habitat基准测试中优于动作标记和动作块基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航研究多关注高级指令推理、记忆和全局地图构建，而低级动作表示相对未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CoFL-S框架，预测机器人局部可视区域上的语言条件流场，通过滚动预测的流场生成连续轨迹；将VLN-CE整段指令和动作序列转换为帧级局部监督，包含子指令、动作、轨迹和密集流场目标；引入连续时间Habitat基准，通过共享速度命令控制器隔离低级动作接口。

Result: 在匹配编码器和训练设置下，CoFL-S在不同规划频率的连续时间Habitat基准中一致优于动作标记和动作块基线；零样本真实世界闭环部署也显示出优势。

Conclusion: CoFL-S通过预测条件流场实现连续轨迹生成，在仿真和真实环境中均表现优异，证明了低级动作表示改进对VLN任务的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [165] [NEUROSYMLAND: Neuro-Symbolic Landing-Site Assessment for Robust and Edge-Deployable UAV Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02277)
*Weixian Qian,Tianyi Yang,Sebastian Schroder,Yao Deng,Jiaohong Yao,Xiao Cheng,Richard Han,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NEUROSYMLAND，一种神经符号着陆点评估系统，结合轻量级感知与显式符号安全推理，通过概率语义场景图和符号约束实现结构化推理。在72个模拟场景中完成61次成功评估，优于四个基线（37-57次成功），并在100次硬件在环试验中验证了边缘设备上的可行性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-only学习方法在地形变化场景下性能退化且缺乏安全决策透明度，现有方法难以在边缘设备上实现鲁棒的着陆点评估。

Method: 构建概率语义场景图（PSSG），结合符号约束（地形平坦度、障碍物间距、空间一致性）进行显式安全推理，在边缘设备上执行轻量级感知与结构化推理流水线。

Result: 72个模拟场景中61次成功评估（优于基线37-57次）；100次硬件在环试验验证了端到端延迟、资源占用等系统级指标，符号推理仅占延迟的很小部分。

Conclusion: 该方法在边缘受限硬件上实现了优于纯视觉方法的鲁棒性和可解释性，证明了神经符号方法在无人机自主着陆场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Safe landing-site assessment in unstructured environments remains a key challenge for autonomous UAV deployment, as vision-only learning approaches often degrade under terrain variability and provide limited transparency in safety decisions. We present NEUROSYMLAND, a neuro-symbolic landing-site assessment system that integrates lightweight perception with explicit safety reasoning. The framework constructs a probabilistic semantic scene graph from onboard visual input and evaluates candidate landing regions using symbolic constraints capturing terrain flatness, obstacle clearance, and spatial consistency, enabling structured reasoning under perceptual uncertainty while maintaining edge-feasible execution. Across 72 simulated landing scenarios spanning diverse terrains, NEUROSYMLAND achieves 61 successful assessments, outperforming four competitive baselines (37-57 successes). To evaluate deployability, we further conduct 100 hardware-in-the-loop trials with randomized initial poses, profiling end-to-end latency, stage-wise execution time, and system-level metrics including CPU/GPU utilization, memory footprint, and power consumption. Results demonstrate improved robustness and interpretability with bounded edge-resource usage. Profiling shows that symbolic reasoning contributes only a small fraction of end-to-end latency, while the main computational cost arises from perception and PSSG construction. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the landing-site assessment stack on edge-constrained UAV hardware, and all source code, datasets, prompts, and symbolic rule refinement examples are released in an open-source repository

</details>


### [166] [Real-Time Visual Intelligence on Low-Cost UAVs: A Modular Approach for Tracking, Scanning, and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02298)
*Andrei-Marian Ungureanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于DJI Tello无人机平台，集成了面部检测、面部识别和单目深度估计三种AI功能，构建了一个低成本、开源的个人助理无人机系统，并通过网页界面实现实时控制与监控，在真实环境中验证了其在受限硬件上运行先进AI技术的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用无人机解决方案成本高、不开放，缺乏面向个人助理场景的低成本、可访问的集成智能系统。

Method: 采用DJI Tello无人机平台，设计模块化架构，集成面部检测、面部识别和单目深度估计三种AI功能；通过Python后端处理视觉数据并运行轻量级神经网络模型，网页前端实现无人机控制与实时视频监控。

Result: 系统在真实世界条件下表现出鲁棒性能，包括人物跟踪、室内扫描和基于虚拟传感器的自主线跟随。

Conclusion: 验证了在受限硬件上部署先进AI技术的可行性，为未来军事、救援和监视任务中的自主无人机研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous drones are rapidly transforming modern warfare and civil applications alike. This paper presents the development of an integrated intelligent drone system designed to serve as a personal assistant. Leveraging the DJI Tello drone platform, we implemented a modular architecture that integrates three core artificial intelligence functionalities: facial detection, facial recognition, and depth estimation from monocular vision. A web-based interface enables seamless drone control and real-time video monitoring, while a Python-based server processes visual data and executes inference pipelines using lightweight neural models optimized for embedded systems. Unlike existing commercial solutions, this system emphasizes accessibility, low-cost hardware, and open-source technologies. The system demonstrates robust performance in real-world conditions, including person tracking, indoor scanning, and autonomous line following using virtual sensors. This project validates the applicability of advanced AI techniques in real-time robotic systems and illustrates the feasibility of deploying them on constrained hardware, providing a foundation for future research in autonomous UAVs for military, rescue, and surveillance missions.

</details>


### [167] [The Moving Eye: Enhancing VLA Spatial Generalization via Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02322)
*Jincheng Tang,Yilong Zhu,Zhengyuan Xie,Jiang-Jiang Liu,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据中心的解决方案，通过混合移动与固定视角的策略来增强VLA模型的空间泛化能力，减少捷径学习带来的虚假相关性。实验表明，该策略优于单纯增加静态视角，且适用于ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t等多种架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在泛化机器人操作中空间泛化能力不足，简单增加视角数量无法解决模型陷入捷径学习（如依赖物体间或相机与机器人基座间的固定相对位姿）的问题。

Method: 采用双臂设置，一支臂执行操作，另一支臂作为移动环境相机。系统评估三种数据分布模式：固定视角、多固定视角和移动视角，提出混合策略——连续相机运动结合多样化静态视角——以减少虚假相关性同时保持训练稳定性。

Result: 混合策略在减少虚假相关性方面表现最佳，使VLA模型能够泛化到未见过的相机位姿和物体配置，而单纯增加静态视角则失败。所有评估模型（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均从该策略中显著受益。

Conclusion: 对捷径学习的易感性和空间泛化困难是跨不同架构的通用特征，混合数据策略是提升VLA空间泛化的有效数据中心解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in generalized robotic manipulation. However, their spatial generalization remains fragile. We argue that simply increasing the number of viewpoints is insufficient. Models often fall into the trap of Shortcut Learning, latching onto spurious correlations (e.g., fixed relative poses between objects or between the camera and robot base) rather than learning true spatial relationships. In this work, we propose a data-centric solution to enhance VLA spatial generalization. We utilize a dual-arm setup where one arm performs manipulation while the other serves as a mobile environmental camera. We systematically evaluate three data distribution patterns: Fixed, Multi-Fixed, and Moving Views. Our findings reveal that a hybrid strategy, combining continuous camera motion with diverse static viewpoints, yields the best performance by substantially reducing spurious correlations while maintaining training stability. Our experiments demonstrate that this strategy mitigates spurious correlations, enabling VLAs to generalize to unseen camera poses and object configurations where simply adding more static viewpoints fails. Crucially, we reveal that the susceptibility to shortcut learning and the struggle with spatial generalization are universal characteristics shared across diverse architectures. Consequently, all evaluated models (ACT, Diffusion, and VLA models including Pi0 and Gr00t) benefit significantly from our mixed data strategy.

</details>


### [168] [HEFT: Heavy-Payload Full-size Humanoid Teleoperation with Privileged Motion Guidance and Windowed Payload Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02332)
*Chenxin Liu,Qingzhou Lu,Guangxiao Yang,Xuanyang Shi,Chenghan Yang,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEFT是一个面向全尺寸人形机器人的重负载远程操作框架，通过特权运动引导（PMG）和窗口化负载课程（WPC）解决大惯性、紧平衡容限下的噪声和漂移问题，在175cm、65kg的L7人形机器人上实现了高达24kg负载下的运动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用运动跟踪与远程操作框架大多在紧凑平台上验证，未涉及真实负载交互，全尺寸人形机器人在重负载下的遥操作尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出HEFT框架，包含两个核心组件：1）Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG)——从可部署的噪声VR参考中学习物理上合理的重建参考；2）Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC)——结合专家引导的负载上限，获取鲁棒的重负载跟踪能力。

Result: 在L7（175cm, 65kg）全尺寸人形机器人上部署，实现了包括转弯、前进/后退行走、深蹲等动作的跟踪，负载最高达24kg。

Conclusion: HEFT有效解决了全尺寸人形机器人在重负载遥操作中因大惯性和紧平衡容限带来的噪声、漂移及重定向误差问题，并充分发挥了其负载潜力。

Abstract: General motion tracking and teleoperation offer a promising path to scalable humanoid skill acquisition, yet most existing frameworks are validated on compact platforms or without real payload interaction, leaving full-size humanoids with real payloads largely unexplored. Scaling to full-size humanoids introduces two compounding challenges: their larger inertia and tighter balance margins make tracking highly sensitive to noise, drift, and retargeting errors from commodity VR trackers, while their payload potential remains largely underutilized. We present HEFT, a heavy-payload full-size humanoid teleoperation framework that addresses both challenges. HEFT learns from deployable noisy VR references with physically plausible reconstructed references through Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG), and uses a Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) with expert-guided payload caps to acquire robust heavy-payload tracking. We deploy HEFT on L7, a 175cm, 65kg humanoid. The robot tracks motions including turns, forward/backward locomotion, and squats under payloads up to 24kg.

</details>


### [169] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo,Dongwon Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACID框架，通过循环动作一致性（用逆动力学模型验证预测过渡的可实现性）改进基于世界模型的决策时规划，在多种任务和模型中一致提升规划效果并减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准规划成本仅检查预测终端状态与目标的接近程度，而不验证中间过渡的可实现性，导致预测轨迹看似合理但实际执行时偏离。

Method: 提出ACID框架，引入循环动作一致性：用逆动力学模型从预测的过渡中反向推断动作，应与原条件动作一致。通过尺度不变的自适应权重将该逐步残差融入规划成本。

Result: 在4个动作条件世界模型和6个任务（刚体/可变形操作、关节控制、视觉导航）上，ACID一致提升规划质量，并在显著减少计算量的情况下达到基线精度。

Conclusion: 循环动作一致性作为一种轻量级的可微约束，能有效甄别不可实现的轨迹，提升决策时规划的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [170] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun,Jiajie Li,Yung-Hsu Yang,Chenyangguang Zhang,Tim Engelbracht,Sunghwan Hong,Cesar Cadena,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIME模型，从被动第一人称视频中学习语言条件驱动的相机运动生成，将自由形式自然语言意图映射为相对SE(3)目标相机位姿，并联合预测观测增益（下一视图应揭示什么）和连续多假设目标位姿，最终实现意图感知的主动感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航和操作策略较多，但语言条件驱动的相机运动作为独立动作类别研究不足；相机视角变化由潜在感知意图驱动，且运动语义粒度多样（从进入房间到检查物体），需要建模这种多意图结构。

Method: 从第一人称视频挖掘多意图相机运动监督数据（配对自然语言意图/观测增益描述与相对SE(3)目标位姿）；提出LIME模型，结合自回归观测增益输出（预测下一视图应揭示什么）与连续流匹配位姿头（表示多假设目标视图）。

Result: 实验表明LIME能从被动人类视频中学习主动选择相机位姿，将普通第一人称记录转化为意图感知主动感知的监督信号，在多种下游机器人任务中有效。

Conclusion: 语言条件相机运动生成是一个可学习的、有价值的任务；LIME通过联合预测观测增益与多假设位姿，能够从被动视频数据中学会意图驱动的主动感知行为，为机器人自主感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [171] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue,Le Xu,Zeyi Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Zhengyi Gu,Xinyang Song,Bofang Jia,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSample是一种用于真实机器人强化学习的物理基础数据增强框架，通过物理 rollout、世界模型生成和策略改进之间的真实-合成闭环，结合策略节奏学习（PPL）来调节训练过程，在接触密集和精确操作任务上将策略成功率提升28%，训练步骤减少59%，并将世界模型视觉保真度提升19.4dB PSNR和0.47 SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能克服模仿学习的演示覆盖限制，但真实机器人部署受限于高昂的交互成本，每次物理 rollout 成本高且仅反映一条行动-结果路径。

Method: 提出WorldSample框架，构建物理rollout、世界模型后训练与策略改进之间的真实-合成闭环；利用后训练的世界模型生成高保真合成转换；引入策略节奏学习（PPL），通过样本选择和调度来平衡有用增强与值函数过估计，缓解幻觉噪声。

Result: 在接触密集和精确操作任务上，策略成功率提升28%，训练步骤减少59%；世界模型视觉保真度提升19.4dB PSNR和0.47 SSIM。

Conclusion: WorldSample通过真实-合成闭环有效同时提升策略性能和世界模型视觉质量，验证了该框架在真实机器人RL中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

</details>


### [172] [Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02466)
*Junhao Shi,Siyin Wang,Xiaopeng Yu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务无关预训练（TAP）框架，将VLA模型的学习解耦为物理能力（如何移动）和语义对齐（做什么）两个目标，通过自监督逆动力学从廉价未标注交互数据中学习可迁移的运动先验，再用极少量专家数据完成语言对齐，在SIMPLER基准上匹配了百万级专家轨迹训练的性能，在真实机器人上对相机扰动具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于专家演示数据的稀缺性，其根本原因在于将物理能力获取和语义对齐两个不同学习目标混为一谈，其中只有后者需要语言监督。

Method: 提出TAP两阶段框架：第一阶段通过自监督逆动力学目标从廉价未标注交互数据（包括离轨轨迹和自主探索）学习可迁移的运动先验；第二阶段用极少量专家数据将运动先验与语言对齐。

Result: 在SIMPLER基准上，TAP匹配了超过100万条专家轨迹训练的性能，比标准行为克隆提升10%；在真实WidowX平台上，相机扰动下TAP保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线降至0%。

Conclusion: 任务无关预训练能够产生鲁棒且可迁移的物理表征，为具身AI提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态的学习控制策略，通过可微分四旋翼动力学和分析策略梯度方法，实现最高10 m/s的敏捷拦截，相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法需要相对位置或距离信息，但这些信息在实际被动单目相机传感器应用中难以获取。

Method: 利用可微分四旋翼动力学的分析策略梯度方法，仅使用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态作为输入。

Result: 在最高10 m/s速度下实现敏捷拦截，相较使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均性能提升30%。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在仅依赖方向信息的条件下仍能有效学习拦截策略，且优于传统简化动力学基线方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [174] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可控神经变分智能体（CNeVA），通过闭式共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报中推断高斯行为潜变量，结合基于混合通道掩码课程训练的整流流轨迹生成器，实现可控且真实的交通模拟，并在Waymo数据集上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交通模拟需要既能模仿记录行为又能沿可解释轴控制的智能体，以便工程师隔离变量、复现特定边缘场景并安全测试自动驾驶系统，现有模型缺乏此可控制能力。

Method: 提出CNeVA框架，核心包括：(1) 闭式共轭变分更新，从每通道折扣回报中推断每智能体高斯行为潜变量；(2) 整流流轨迹生成器，基于混合通道掩码课程训练实现无分类器引导；(3) 软合格门控，用平滑指数衰减替代硬二值阈值以保留梯度信号。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上，CNeVA达到具有竞争力的真实感，同时展现可解释的每通道可控性；速度和加速度控制产生单调响应且无失速奖励黑客；软合格门控引入使安全可控性单调且显著；实现了上下文残差回报度量下的可控制地图合规性。

Conclusion: 可控制指标必须与物理合理性护栏一同解读，以避免奖励黑客混淆；CNeVA提供了比排名更高的纯模仿模型更丰富的可控性。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [175] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Embodied.cpp，一个面向具身AI模型的便携式C++推理运行时，通过五层架构设计实现多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展接口，在VLA模型和世界-动作模型上取得高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI模型（VLA和WAM）部署碎片化，依赖模型特定的Python栈和后端假设，缺乏针对具身场景（闭环控制中的多速率执行、异构硬件上延迟优先的batch-1推理、可扩展的具身接口）的专用推理运行时。

Method: 通过分析代表性VLA和WAM架构，抽取共享执行路径，设计五层架构：输入适配器(input adapters)、序列构建器(sequence builders)、骨干执行(backbone execution)、头插件(head plugins)和部署适配器(deployment adapters)。支持模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理、可扩展算子与I/O，单个后端抽象统一异构设备/机器人/模拟器部署。

Result: 在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型上实现闭环执行，任务成功率分别达100.0%和91.0%；在基于LingBot-VA Transformer块的WAM基准上将块内存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时显著提升部署效率，能够支持多种具身模型架构的统一高效部署。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [176] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-WAM是一个视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一流匹配框架联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，在六项接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率达71.67%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要策略对局部变形、压力、滑移和摩擦等触觉线索做出反应，但这些线索在时间上稀疏且视觉观测中不可见。现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测馈入动作预测，很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉变形动力学。

Method: 提出VT-WAM，包含两个关键组件：(1) 非对称混合Transformer注意力，桥接首帧视觉锚点与时间触觉动力学；(2) 接触门控的动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导，鼓励动作查询在接触阶段依赖触觉证据。所有预测在统一流匹配框架中联合训练。

Result: 在六项真实世界接触丰富操作任务中，VT-WAM平均成功率达71.67%，超过Fast-WAM 26.67%和OmniVTLA 35.84%。消融实验表明建模触觉变形动力学和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对接触丰富任务均至关重要。

Conclusion: VT-WAM通过显式建模触觉变形动力学并引导接触阶段的触觉注意力，有效提升了接触丰富操作任务的性能，验证了触觉动力学建模在机器人操作中的重要性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Insights from GitHub Community on the Matter Standard: Developer Perspectives and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01494)
*Muhammad Hassan,Carl Gunter,Susan Landau,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Project CHIP GitHub仓库中超过13,000个issue，利用主题建模和定性分析，识别出开发者在实现和集成Matter物联网标准时面临的四类主要问题：测试、互操作性、开发、平台与网络，揭示了系统性的技术和集成挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matter标准旨在解决IoT领域长期存在的互操作性问题，但开发者在实际工作中使用该标准的体验和遇到的困难尚未被充分研究。

Method: 对Project CHIP GitHub仓库中超过13,000个issue进行主题建模和定性分析。

Result: 识别出四类核心问题领域：测试、互操作性、开发、平台与网络，并描述了这些问题在代码库和工具演变中的具体表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了系统性的技术和集成挑战，为改进Matter的测试基础设施、跨供应商指导和文档提供了具体机会，有助于标准的成熟发展。

Abstract: Matter seeks to resolve longstanding interoperability problems in the Internet of Things (IoT), yet little is known about how developers experience the standard in day to day work. This paper examines over 13,000 issues from the official Project CHIP GitHub repository to understand the kinds of problems contributors report when implementing and integrating Matter. Using topic modeling and qualitative analysis, we identify four recurring areas of concern, Testing, Interoperability, Development, and Platform and Network, and describe how they manifest in the evolution of the codebase and tooling. The findings reveal systematic technical and integration challenges and point to concrete opportunities to refine Matter's test infrastructure, cross vendor guidance, and documentation as the standard continues to mature.

</details>


### [178] [Benchmarking Code Improvement with Progressive, Adaptive, and Interactive Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01360)
*Cuong Chi Le,Aashish Yadavally,Minh Le-Anh,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAIR-Bench 是一个渐进式自适应基准，用于评估LLM通过反馈引导改进代码的能力，使用渐进式提示协议（包含失败区域控制和提示深度控制）来细粒度衡量模型的修复、泛化、保持和辅助需求能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅使用二元功能正确性（通过/失败测试套件），忽略了部分进展、反馈利用、回归以及模型改进代码的优化轨迹，缺乏对代码改进能力的细粒度评估。

Method: 提出PAIR-Bench基准，采用渐进式提示（progressive hinting）协议，包含两个控制维度：失败区域控制（failure-region control）决定反馈目标，提示深度控制（hint-depth control）决定透露信息量。评估修复轨迹而非仅最终结果。

Result: 能够测量模型是否修复目标失败、是否泛化到提示之外、是否保持正确行为以及需要多少辅助信息。

Conclusion: 提供比传统通过/失败二元评估更细粒度的LLM代码改进能力评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically evaluated on code generation and program repair using binary functional correctness: a generated program or patch either passes or fails a test suite. This protocol is simple but coarse, as it ignores partial progress, feedback use, regressions, and the refinement trajectory through which models often improve code. We introduce PAIR-Bench, a progressive and adaptive benchmark for evaluating code improvement: transforming an incorrect or incomplete program into a more correct one through feedback-guided refinement. PAIR-Bench uses progressive hinting, a structured feedback protocol with two controls. Failure-region control determines what the feedback targets by grouping hidden failing tests into failure scenarios, while hint-depth control determines how much repair-relevant information is revealed, from coarse symptoms to implementation-level guidance. This design enables PAIR-Bench to measure whether a model repairs targeted failures, generalizes beyond the hint, preserves already-correct behavior, and how much assistance it requires. By evaluating repair trajectories progressive metrics rather than only final pass/fail outcomes, PAIR-Bench provides a finer-grained assessment of LLM code-improvement capability.

</details>


### [179] [A global predicted-fMRI drive signal from TRIBE does not predict YouTube replay heatmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01400)
*Barada Sahu,Shivesh Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于TRIBE模型的深度多模态脑编码预测的fMRI响应与YouTube视频的"最常重播"热力图之间无显著相关性（部分相关系数仅+0.058，p=0.23），表明脑编码模型预测的神经信号无法预测行为参与度，且音乐视频中观察到的中等相关性源自体裁特定的开场/重放伪影而非内容预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度多模态脑编码模型预测的神经信号是否能够预测自然视频观看中的行为参与度（即观众重播行为），从而检验脑编码模型从神经预测到行为预测的泛化能力。

Method: 使用2025年Algonauts脑编码挑战赛冠军模型TRIBE（Llama-3.2+V-JEPA2+Wav2Vec-BERT）处理48个YouTube视频，将预测的皮层响应通过全局场功率降维为每秒参与度曲线，与视频的"最常重播"热力图进行位置控制偏相关分析，并对比响度和运动基线。

Result: 总体位置控制偏相关为+0.058（95% CI [-0.04, 0.15]；t(47)=1.21, p=0.23），与零无显著差异；未显著高于响度（+0.04，配对p=0.74）和运动基线；音乐视频中的中等相关值由体裁特定的开场/重放伪影导致，不具泛化性；在六种皮层网络读出和自相关保持置换检验下均不显著。

Conclusion: 深度脑编码模型预测的神经信号无法预测YouTube视频的行为参与度（重播行为），表明脑编码模型在从神经预测到行为预测的泛化上存在局限性。

Abstract: Deep multimodal brain-encoding models now predict fMRI responses to naturalistic video with high accuracy. Whether their predicted neural signals also forecast behavioral engagement is unknown. We run TRIBE, the winning model of the 2025 Algonauts brain-encoding challenge (Llama-3.2 + V-JEPA2 + Wav2Vec-BERT), on 48 YouTube videos and reduce its predicted cortical response to a per-second engagement curve, the global field power. Correlated against each video's "most replayed" heatmap, a passively-collected proxy for which moments viewers return to, the curve shows no evidence of predicting re-watch behavior. The pooled position-controlled partial correlation is +0.058 (95% CI [-0.04, 0.15]; one-sample t(47)=1.21, p=0.23), indistinguishable from zero and not significantly above simple loudness and motion baselines (loudness +0.04, paired p=0.74). The raw correlation is also near zero; the moderate values reported for music videos reflect a genre-specific intro/onset-replay artifact rather than content prediction, and do not generalize. The null holds across six cortical-network readouts and under an autocorrelation-preserving permutation test. We release the code, the video-ID manifest, and an acquisition method that works despite YouTube's SABR-only streaming.

</details>


### [180] [GPUAlert: A Zero-Instrumentation Process-Boundary Monitor for Diagnosing GPU Training-Job Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01409)
*Parv Agarwal,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GPUAlert是一个命令行包装器，可在不修改训练脚本的情况下监控GPU训练作业，通过进程边界监控、预启动日志保证、通知器隔离和非静默工件预算等可靠性原语，在作业完成时发送包含分类故障原因、持久日志和输出工件的结构化邮件通知。基于474个GPU训练日志（15种故障类别）的实验表明，有序规则分类器达到0.997 macro-F1，开销约3ms/作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU训练作业频繁失败（大型生产集群中约40%），但运维人员通常要数小时后重新连接才能得知失败；实验跟踪器需要编辑训练脚本并维持云连接；调度器的邮件钩子仅发送单行状态，无故障原因和日志。缺乏一种无需修改脚本、能自动捕获故障原因和日志的监控工具。

Method: 提出GPUAlert命令行包装器，在进程边界监控训练命令。核心包含三个可靠性原语：(1) 预启动日志保证——在子进程可能崩溃前建立持久日志目标；(2) 通知器隔离——使包装器的退出码成为子进程状态的纯函数，无论邮件发送是否成功；(3) 非静默工件预算——限制附件大小但不会静默丢弃输出。采用有序规则分类器对故障原因进行分类。

Result: 在12个硬件可复现的故障类别上，有序规则分类器达到0.997 macro-F1，对比无序关键词匹配0.830和退出码检查0.133。包装器开销恒定约3ms/作业；预启动保证在shell重定向无输出时仍保留日志；在所有15种故障模式下，即使SMTP中继不可达，包装器仍返回子进程的原始退出码。

Conclusion: GPUAlert能够在不修改训练脚本的情况下，有效监控GPU训练作业失败，提供结构化故障原因和日志。其三个可靠性原语确保了日志持久性、退出码正确性和输出完整性。该方法显著优于传统的退出码检查和关键词匹配方法。

Abstract: GPU training jobs fail often, roughly two in five on large production clusters, yet the operator typically learns of a failure only by reconnecting hours later. Experiment trackers require editing the training script and maintaining a cloud connection; the scheduler's mail hook delivers a single status line with no cause and no logs. GPUAlert is a command-line wrapper that monitors any training command at the process boundary, and with no change to that command, emails a structured notification on completion carrying a classified failure cause, durable logs, and output artifacts. The tool is organized around three reliability primitives: a pre-launch log guarantee that establishes the durable destination before the child process can crash, notifier isolation that makes the wrapper's exit code a pure function of the child's status regardless of whether the email succeeds, and a non-silent artifact budget that bounds attachment size without ever dropping output silently. We release a labelled corpus of 474 GPU training logs across 15 failure classes and a reproducible evaluation harness. On the twelve hardware-reproduced classes, the ordered-rule classifier reaches 0.997 macro-F1, against 0.830 for unordered keyword matching and 0.133 for exit-code inspection. Wrapper overhead is a constant approximately 3ms per job; the pre-launch guarantee preserves a log where a shell redirect yields nothing; and across all 15 failure modes the wrapper returns the child's exit code unchanged even when the SMTP relay is unreachable.

</details>


### [181] [Adoption and Impact of Command-Line AI Coding Agents: A Study of Microsoft's Early 2026 Rollout of Claude Code and GitHub Copilot CLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01418)
*Emerson Murphy-Hill,Jenna Butler,Alexandra Savelieva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 微软在2026年初对数千名工程师的调研发现：CLI编码代理（如Claude Code和Copilot CLI）的首次使用主要通过社交网络传播，留存率与工程师的编码活动量而非人口统计学特征更相关，采用者合并的PR数量比正常情况下多约24%。这一提升在四个月的研究窗口内持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在大规模推广代理型命令行工具时，需要了解谁会尝试使用、谁会持续使用、以及这些工具产生的输出是否值得其高昂成本（年度token支出可达数百万美元），误判采用率、留存率或影响可能导致昂贵的推广却无法提升工程效率。

Method: 在微软2026年初的推广中，对数万名工程师进行研究，通过社交网络分析追踪首次使用传播路径，关联编码活动数据与人口统计学特征分析留存因素，并以合并的PR数量作为输出代理指标评估影响。

Result: 首次使用主要通过社交网络传播；留存率与编码活动量相关，与人口统计学特征无关；采用者合并PR数量比正常情况下多约24%，且该提升在四个月窗口内持续。

Conclusion: CLI编码代理既非被均匀采用，也非仅仅是新颖性效应；组织应将可见的同伴使用作为推广策略的核心。

Abstract: Organizations rolling out agentic command line tools like Anthropic's Claude Code and GitHub's Copilot CLI need to know who will try them, who will keep using them, and whether the tools produce enough output to justify their cost. At organizational scale, token spend can run into millions of dollars annually, so misreading adoption, retention, or impact can make a rollout expensive without changing engineering velocity. Studying tens of thousands of engineers at Microsoft over its early-2026 rollout, we find that first use spread primarily through social networks, retention was associated more with engineers' coding activity than with demographics, and adopters merged roughly 24% more pull requests than they would have otherwise. We use merged pull requests as our proxy for output -- acknowledging that a merged PR is not the same as the value it delivers -- and the lift persists across our four-month window. These results suggest that CLI coding agents are neither uniformly adopted nor mere novelty effects and that organizations should treat visible peer use as central to rollout strategy.

</details>


### [182] [Risk Architecture for AI-Native Engineering Teams: An Organizational Framework for Agentic System Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01421)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了从纯软件工程团队到AI原生团队转型过程中，现有软件风险管理框架的适用性退化问题。作者发现，传统框架在应对概率性输出、自主多步行动和静默变化风险表面时存在系统性缺陷，特别是在AI原生团队与依赖其输出的确定性系统之间的组织边界上，会出现最严重且覆盖最差的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件风险管理框架（如按特性所有权、按严重性升级、按测试覆盖率保证）隐含假设确定性行为、离散可审计变更事件和清晰的组件-所有者映射，而构建和运行Agentic AI系统的团队同时违反了这三项假设。现有AI风险文献要么从政策框架层面（如NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001）要么从威胁分类层面（如OWASP的Agentic AI指南）处理风险，但未涉及工程管理者（EM）实际操作的层面——角色、决策权和升级结构。

Method: 本文提出：(i) 一个七维度画像，区分纯软件工程团队、混合团队和AI原生团队；(ii) 一个六聚类失效模式分类法，包括一个此前未阐明的聚类——依赖边界确定性不匹配；(iii) 一个综合框架充分性评估方法，评分每个画像的风险架构对定义场景集的检测、包含和升级能力。评估基于框架充分性而非人类行为，得出推导性而非观察性的覆盖声明。

Result: 随着团队从纯软件工程向AI原生操作转变，覆盖能力呈退化趋势，中位数上单调递减，在AI原生步骤上出现未覆盖的高后果失效数量急剧增加。退化集中在特定的失效模式类别中。最严重、覆盖最差的失效并非出现在AI原生团队内部，而是在其概率性输出被确定性假设的依赖方消费时的组织边界处。

Conclusion: 现有软件风险管理框架不适用于AI原生团队，且最危险的风险缺口存在于组织间接口处。需要为工程管理者设计新的风险架构，以处理概率性与确定性系统之间的边界不匹配问题。

Abstract: Engineering management research has produced mature frameworks for software risk: ownership by feature, escalation by severity, and assurance by test coverage. These frameworks implicitly assume deterministic behavior, discrete and auditable change events, and clear component-to-owner mappings. Teams that build and operate agentic AI systems violate all three assumptions at once: outputs are probabilistic, systems take autonomous multi-step actions, and the risk surface mutates silently between deployments. Existing AI risk literature addresses this from above (policy frameworks such as the NIST AI RMF and ISO/IEC 42001) or below (threat taxonomies such as OWASP's agentic AI guidance), but not at the layer where an engineering manager (EM) operates: roles, decision rights, and escalation structures. This paper contributes (i) a seven-dimension profile distinguishing pure software-engineering, hybrid, and AI-native teams; (ii) a six-cluster failure-mode taxonomy including a previously unarticulated cluster, dependency-boundary determinism mismatch; and (iii) a synthetic framework-adequacy methodology scoring how well each profile's risk architecture detects, contains, and escalates a defined scenario set. Because the object of study is framework adequacy rather than human behavior, the evaluation yields derived rather than observed coverage claims. Coverage degrades as teams move from pure software engineering to AI-native operation, monotonically in the median and abruptly in the count of uncovered, high-consequence failures appearing only at the AI-native step. The degradation concentrates in specific failure-mode categories, and the most severe, least-covered failures arise not inside AI-native teams but at the organizational boundary where their probabilistic outputs are consumed by determinism-assuming dependencies.

</details>


### [183] [From Anatomy to Smells: An Empirical Study of SKILL.md in Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01456)
*David Boram Hong,Aaron Imani,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM智能体技能文件（SKILL.md）的编写实践，通过对238个真实技能进行定性分析、29篇文献的多声部综述，提出了语义组件分类体系和技能坏味概念，发现超过99%的技能文件存在坏味且难以自行消除，揭示出实际编写与推荐实践之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills被广泛采用，但SKILL.md文件的编写实践缺乏系统研究，现有编写指南是否被遵循尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 对238个真实技能文件进行定性分析，归纳语义组件分类体系（13个高层级、44个低层级）；2) 对29篇文献进行多声部文献综述，提炼编写最佳实践并定义技能坏味；3) 开发自动化检测工具并应用于真实技能。

Result: 超过99%的SKILL.md文件至少包含一个技能坏味，且坏味一旦引入便很少随技能演化而消失。

Conclusion: 实际编写实践与推荐实践之间存在显著差距，亟需开发自动化技术来修复技能坏味，同时提高开发者对这一新兴质量问题的认识。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide on-demand domain knowledge to LLM agents without requiring model retraining. Each Agent Skill is defined by a mandatory SKILL.md file containing metadata and an unstructured Markdown body whose contents are left entirely to the skill author. Despite the rapid adoption of Agent Skills, little is known about how these files are authored or whether existing authoring guidelines are followed in practice. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of SKILL.md files as a software artifact. We qualitatively analyze 238 real-world skills and derive a taxonomy of 13 higher-level and 44 lower-level semantic components. We then conduct a multivocal literature review of 29 sources to identify best practices for authoring SKILL.md files and introduce skill smells as violations of these practices. Finally, we develop an automated detector and apply it to real-world skills, finding that over 99% of SKILL.md files contain at least one skill smell, and once introduced, skill smells rarely disappear as skills evolve. These findings reveal a substantial gap between recommended and actual authoring practices, motivating the development of automated techniques to remediate skill smells while increasing developer awareness of this emerging quality issue.

</details>


### [184] [Kani: A Model Checker for Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01504)
